using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Data;
using System.Data.Entity;
using System.Data.SqlClient;
using System.Linq;
using GTSErpSystem.Helper;

namespace GTSErpSystem.BLL.Orders;

internal class Class_Orders
{
	private int CachCode = 0;

	private DataTable dtCurrentTable = new DataTable();

	private GTSdbEntities db = new GTSdbEntities();

	private int DecimalNum = LoginDetails.DecimalNum;

	private SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection("Data Source=" + LoginDetails.nameServer + ";Initial Catalog=" + LoginDetails.databaseServer + ";Persist Security Info=True;User ID=" + LoginDetails.userNameServer + ";Password=" + LoginDetails.passServer);

	public List<Account_AccountType> GetAccountType()
	{
		return ((IEnumerable<Account_AccountType>)db.Account_AccountType).ToList();
	}

	public string GetAccountNameById(int id)
	{
		return (from a in (IQueryable<Account_AccountType>)db.Account_AccountType
			where a.ID == id
			select a.Name).FirstOrDefault();
	}

	public string GetCostCenterByID(int id)
	{
		return (from x in (IQueryable<Account_CostCenters>)db.Account_CostCenters
			where x.CostCentersID == (int?)id
			select x.CostCentersName).FirstOrDefault();
	}

	public string GetBonuceByID(int id)
	{
		return (from x in (IQueryable<Contract_Bounce>)db.Contract_Bounce
			where x.ID == (int?)id
			select x.Name).FirstOrDefault();
	}

	public List<User_SellPrice> GetUser_SellPrice()
	{
		return ((IQueryable<User_SellPrice>)db.User_SellPrice).Where((User_SellPrice x) => x.ID <= 3).ToList();
	}

	public List<Select_SearchAccountCostCenter_Result> GetCostCenters()
	{
		return ((IEnumerable<Select_SearchAccountCostCenter_Result>)db.Select_SearchAccountCostCenter(Convert.ToInt32(LoginDetails.BranchID))).ToList();
	}

	public List<Order_Orders> GetPurchases()
	{
		GTSdbEntities gTSdbEntities = new GTSdbEntities();
		return ((IQueryable<Order_Orders>)gTSdbEntities.Order_Orders).Where((Order_Orders x) => x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).ToList();
	}

	public List<Order_Orders> GetPurchases(int Code)
	{
		GTSdbEntities gTSdbEntities = new GTSdbEntities();
		return ((IQueryable<Order_Orders>)gTSdbEntities.Order_Orders).Where((Order_Orders x) => x.PurBranchID == (int?)Code && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).ToList();
	}

	public Item_Items GetItems(int itemid)
	{
		GTSdbEntities gTSdbEntities = new GTSdbEntities();
		try
		{
			return ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).FirstOrDefault((Item_Items x) => x.ItemId == itemid);
		}
		finally
		{
			((IDisposable)gTSdbEntities)?.Dispose();
		}
	}

	public Printer GetPrinter(int itemid)
	{
		int PrintID = 0;
		int CatID = Convert.ToInt32(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.ItemId == itemid).FirstOrDefault().Category_ID);
		bool flag = true;
		PrintID = Convert.ToInt32(((IQueryable<Item_Groups>)db.Item_Groups).Where((Item_Groups x) => x.ID == CatID).FirstOrDefault().PrinterID);
		return ((IQueryable<Printer>)db.Printers).Where((Printer x) => x.ID == PrintID).FirstOrDefault();
	}

	public Item_Items GetItemsMizan(string BarcodeMizan)
	{
		GTSdbEntities gTSdbEntities = new GTSdbEntities();
		return ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.SmallBarCode1 == BarcodeMizan || x.SmallBarCode2 == BarcodeMizan || x.SmallBarCode3 == BarcodeMizan || x.MediumBarCode1 == BarcodeMizan || x.MediumBarCode2 == BarcodeMizan || x.MediumBarCode3 == BarcodeMizan || x.BigBarCode1 == BarcodeMizan || x.BigBarCode2 == BarcodeMizan || x.BigBarCode3 == BarcodeMizan).FirstOrDefault();
	}

	public ItemMatchResult GetItemsOnly(string Code)
	{
		GTSdbEntities gTSdbEntities = new GTSdbEntities();
		try
		{
			Item_Items item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).FirstOrDefault((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Code);
			if (item_Items != null)
			{
				return new ItemMatchResult
				{
					Item = item_Items,
					MatchedField = "Item_code"
				};
			}
			item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).FirstOrDefault((Item_Items x) => x.SmallBarCode1 == Code);
			if (item_Items != null)
			{
				return new ItemMatchResult
				{
					Item = item_Items,
					MatchedField = "SmallBarCode1"
				};
			}
			item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).FirstOrDefault((Item_Items x) => x.SmallBarCode2 == Code);
			if (item_Items != null)
			{
				return new ItemMatchResult
				{
					Item = item_Items,
					MatchedField = "SmallBarCode2"
				};
			}
			item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).FirstOrDefault((Item_Items x) => x.SmallBarCode3 == Code);
			if (item_Items != null)
			{
				return new ItemMatchResult
				{
					Item = item_Items,
					MatchedField = "SmallBarCode3"
				};
			}
			item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).FirstOrDefault((Item_Items x) => x.MediumBarCode1 == Code);
			if (item_Items != null)
			{
				return new ItemMatchResult
				{
					Item = item_Items,
					MatchedField = "MediumBarCode1"
				};
			}
			item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).FirstOrDefault((Item_Items x) => x.MediumBarCode2 == Code);
			if (item_Items != null)
			{
				return new ItemMatchResult
				{
					Item = item_Items,
					MatchedField = "MediumBarCode2"
				};
			}
			item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).FirstOrDefault((Item_Items x) => x.MediumBarCode3 == Code);
			if (item_Items != null)
			{
				return new ItemMatchResult
				{
					Item = item_Items,
					MatchedField = "MediumBarCode3"
				};
			}
			item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).FirstOrDefault((Item_Items x) => x.BigBarCode1 == Code);
			if (item_Items != null)
			{
				return new ItemMatchResult
				{
					Item = item_Items,
					MatchedField = "BigBarCode1"
				};
			}
			item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).FirstOrDefault((Item_Items x) => x.BigBarCode2 == Code);
			if (item_Items != null)
			{
				return new ItemMatchResult
				{
					Item = item_Items,
					MatchedField = "BigBarCode2"
				};
			}
			item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).FirstOrDefault((Item_Items x) => x.BigBarCode3 == Code);
			if (item_Items != null)
			{
				return new ItemMatchResult
				{
					Item = item_Items,
					MatchedField = "BigBarCode3"
				};
			}
			return null;
		}
		finally
		{
			((IDisposable)gTSdbEntities)?.Dispose();
		}
	}

	public int InsertItems_Complant(DataTable Items, string ItemCode)
	{
		int result = 0;
		foreach (DataRow row in Items.Rows)
		{
			if (row["كود_الصنف"].ToString() != "")
			{
				Item_ItemComponent item_ItemComponent = new Item_ItemComponent();
				string Itemcod = row["كود_الصنف"].ToString();
				string value = Convert.ToString(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == ItemCode).FirstOrDefault().ItemId);
				string value2 = Convert.ToString(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Itemcod).FirstOrDefault().ItemId);
				int ItemRowChek = Convert.ToInt32(value2);
				Item_Items item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items z) => z.ItemId == ItemRowChek).FirstOrDefault();
				if (item_Items != null)
				{
					item_ItemComponent.ItemID_Master = Convert.ToInt32(value);
					item_ItemComponent.ItemID_Complant = Convert.ToInt32(item_Items.ItemId);
					item_ItemComponent.Amount = Convert.ToDecimal(row["الكمية"].ToString());
					string nmae = row["الوحدة"].ToString();
					item_ItemComponent.ItemUnitID = GetUnitIdByName(nmae);
					item_ItemComponent.SmallUnitPrice = Convert.ToDecimal(row["م.التكلفة"].ToString());
					item_ItemComponent.TotalPrice = Math.Round(Convert.ToDecimal(row["الاجمالى"].ToString()), DecimalNum);
					item_ItemComponent.ItemUnitType = row["أسم_الوحدة"].ToString();
					item_ItemComponent.StoreID = Convert.ToInt32(row["رقم المخزن"]);
					db.Item_ItemComponent.Add(item_ItemComponent);
					result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
				}
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	public int ItemQuantityIn(int ItemID, decimal Quantity, string unitType, int StoreID, int itemType)
	{
		GTSdbEntities gTSdbEntities = new GTSdbEntities();
		int result = 0;
		if (ItemID > 0)
		{
			if (itemType == 1)
			{
				ItemQuantity itemQuantity = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)ItemID && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
				Item_Items item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.ItemId == ItemID).FirstOrDefault();
				if (itemQuantity != null)
				{
					ItemQuantity itemQuantity2 = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)ItemID && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
					if (itemQuantity2 != null)
					{
						if (unitType == "الصغرى")
						{
							itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance -= (decimal?)Quantity;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (unitType == "المتوسطة")
						{
							decimal? currentBalance = itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance;
							decimal value = Quantity;
							decimal? convertMediumUnit = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
							itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance = currentBalance - (decimal?)value * convertMediumUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (unitType == "الكبرى")
						{
							decimal? currentBalance2 = itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance;
							decimal value = Quantity;
							decimal? convertMediumUnit2 = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
							itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance = currentBalance2 - (decimal?)value * convertMediumUnit2 * item_Items.ConvertBigUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
					}
					return result;
				}
				ItemQuantity itemQuantity3 = new ItemQuantity();
				int itemQuantityID = Convert.ToInt32(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Select((ItemQuantity p) => p.ItemQuantityID).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Max()) + 1;
				itemQuantity3.ItemQuantityID = itemQuantityID;
				itemQuantity3.ItemID = ItemID;
				if (unitType == "الصغرى")
				{
					itemQuantity3.CurrentBalance = -Quantity;
				}
				if (unitType == "المتوسطة")
				{
					decimal value = -Quantity;
					decimal? currentBalance = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
					itemQuantity3.CurrentBalance = (decimal?)value * currentBalance;
				}
				if (unitType == "الكبرى")
				{
					decimal value = -Quantity;
					decimal? convertMediumUnit3 = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
					itemQuantity3.CurrentBalance = (decimal?)value * convertMediumUnit3 * item_Items.ConvertBigUnit;
				}
				itemQuantity3.BeginningInventory = 0m;
				itemQuantity3.BeginningInventoryPrice = 0m;
				itemQuantity3.OpeningBalance = 0m;
				itemQuantity3.StoreID = StoreID;
				gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities.Add(itemQuantity3);
				result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
			}
			if (itemType == 3)
			{
				List<Item_ItemComponent> list = ((IQueryable<Item_ItemComponent>)gTSdbEntities.Item_ItemComponent).Where((Item_ItemComponent x) => x.ItemID_Master == (int?)ItemID).ToList();
				Item_Items item_Items2 = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.ItemId == ItemID).FirstOrDefault();
				foreach (Item_ItemComponent item in list)
				{
					Item_Items item_Items3 = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => (int?)x.ItemId == item.ItemID_Complant).FirstOrDefault();
					ItemQuantity itemQuantity4 = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == item.ItemID_Complant && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
					if (itemQuantity4 != null)
					{
						ItemQuantity itemQuantity5 = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == item.ItemID_Complant && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
						if (itemQuantity5 == null)
						{
							continue;
						}
						if (unitType == "الصغرى")
						{
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance -= item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance -= item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance -= item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
						}
						if (unitType == "المتوسطة")
						{
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance -= item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance -= item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance -= item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
						}
						if (unitType == "الكبرى")
						{
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance -= item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance -= item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance -= item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
						}
						continue;
					}
					ItemQuantity itemQuantity6 = new ItemQuantity();
					int itemQuantityID2 = Convert.ToInt32(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Select((ItemQuantity p) => p.ItemQuantityID).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Max()) + 1;
					itemQuantity6.ItemQuantityID = itemQuantityID2;
					itemQuantity6.ItemID = item.ItemID_Complant;
					if (unitType == "الصغرى")
					{
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = -item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = -(item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit);
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = -(item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit);
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
					}
					if (unitType == "المتوسطة")
					{
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = -(item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit);
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = -(item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit);
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = -(item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit);
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
					}
					if (unitType == "الكبرى")
					{
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = -(item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit);
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = -(item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit);
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = -(item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit);
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
					}
					itemQuantity6.BeginningInventory = 0m;
					itemQuantity6.BeginningInventoryPrice = 0m;
					itemQuantity6.OpeningBalance = 0m;
					itemQuantity6.StoreID = item.StoreID;
					gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities.Add(itemQuantity6);
					result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
				}
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	public int ItemQuantityOut(int ItemID, decimal Quantity, string unitType, int StoreID, int itemType)
	{
		GTSdbEntities gTSdbEntities = new GTSdbEntities();
		int result = 0;
		if (ItemID > 0)
		{
			if (itemType == 1)
			{
				ItemQuantity itemQuantity = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)ItemID && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
				Item_Items item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.ItemId == ItemID).FirstOrDefault();
				if (itemQuantity != null)
				{
					ItemQuantity itemQuantity2 = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)ItemID && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
					if (itemQuantity2 != null)
					{
						if (unitType == "الصغرى")
						{
							itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance += (decimal?)Quantity;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (unitType == "المتوسطة")
						{
							decimal? currentBalance = itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance;
							decimal value = Quantity;
							decimal? convertMediumUnit = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
							itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance = currentBalance + (decimal?)value * convertMediumUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (unitType == "الكبرى")
						{
							decimal? currentBalance2 = itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance;
							decimal value = Quantity;
							decimal? convertMediumUnit2 = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
							itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance = currentBalance2 + (decimal?)value * convertMediumUnit2 * item_Items.ConvertBigUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
					}
					return result;
				}
				ItemQuantity itemQuantity3 = new ItemQuantity();
				int itemQuantityID = Convert.ToInt32(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Select((ItemQuantity p) => p.ItemQuantityID).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Max()) + 1;
				itemQuantity3.ItemQuantityID = itemQuantityID;
				itemQuantity3.ItemID = ItemID;
				if (unitType == "الصغرى")
				{
					itemQuantity3.CurrentBalance = Quantity;
				}
				if (unitType == "المتوسطة")
				{
					decimal value = Quantity;
					decimal? currentBalance = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
					itemQuantity3.CurrentBalance = (decimal?)value * currentBalance;
				}
				if (unitType == "الكبرى")
				{
					decimal value = Quantity;
					decimal? convertMediumUnit3 = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
					itemQuantity3.CurrentBalance = (decimal?)value * convertMediumUnit3 * item_Items.ConvertBigUnit;
				}
				itemQuantity3.BeginningInventory = 0m;
				itemQuantity3.BeginningInventoryPrice = 0m;
				itemQuantity3.OpeningBalance = 0m;
				itemQuantity3.StoreID = StoreID;
				gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities.Add(itemQuantity3);
				result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
			}
			if (itemType == 3)
			{
				List<Item_ItemComponent> list = ((IQueryable<Item_ItemComponent>)gTSdbEntities.Item_ItemComponent).Where((Item_ItemComponent x) => x.ItemID_Master == (int?)ItemID).ToList();
				Item_Items item_Items2 = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.ItemId == ItemID).FirstOrDefault();
				foreach (Item_ItemComponent item in list)
				{
					Item_Items item_Items3 = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => (int?)x.ItemId == item.ItemID_Complant).FirstOrDefault();
					ItemQuantity itemQuantity4 = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == item.ItemID_Complant && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
					if (itemQuantity4 != null)
					{
						ItemQuantity itemQuantity5 = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == item.ItemID_Complant && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
						if (itemQuantity5 == null)
						{
							continue;
						}
						if (unitType == "الصغرى")
						{
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance += item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance += item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance += item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
						}
						if (unitType == "المتوسطة")
						{
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance += item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance += item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance += item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
						}
						if (unitType == "الكبرى")
						{
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance += item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance += item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance += item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
						}
						continue;
					}
					ItemQuantity itemQuantity6 = new ItemQuantity();
					int itemQuantityID2 = Convert.ToInt32(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Select((ItemQuantity p) => p.ItemQuantityID).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Max()) + 1;
					itemQuantity6.ItemQuantityID = itemQuantityID2;
					itemQuantity6.ItemID = item.ItemID_Complant;
					if (unitType == "الصغرى")
					{
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
					}
					if (unitType == "المتوسطة")
					{
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
					}
					if (unitType == "الكبرى")
					{
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
					}
					itemQuantity6.BeginningInventory = 0m;
					itemQuantity6.BeginningInventoryPrice = 0m;
					itemQuantity6.OpeningBalance = 0m;
					itemQuantity6.StoreID = StoreID;
					gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities.Add(itemQuantity6);
					result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
				}
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

			where x.ID == StoreID
			select x.Store_Name).FirstOrDefault();
	}

	public decimal GetQantityForItems(string itemCode, string unit)
	{
		decimal num = 0m;
		int Itemid = (from x in GetItems(itemCode)
			select x.ItemId).FirstOrDefault();
		decimal num2 = Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)Itemid).Sum((ItemQuantity x) => x.OpeningBalance));
		decimal num3 = Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)Itemid).Sum((ItemQuantity x) => x.CurrentBalance));
		num = num2 + num3;
		if (unit == "المتوسطة")
		{
			num /= Convert.ToDecimal((from x in GetItems(itemCode)
				select x.ConvertMediumUnit).FirstOrDefault());
		}
		if (unit == "الكبرى")
		{
			num /= Convert.ToDecimal((from x in GetItems(itemCode)
				select x.ConvertMediumUnit).FirstOrDefault());
			num /= Convert.ToDecimal((from x in GetItems(itemCode)
				select x.ConvertBigUnit).FirstOrDefault());
		}
		return num;
	}

	public decimal GetQantityForItems(string itemCode, string unit, int storeid)
	{
		decimal num = 0m;
		int Itemid = (from x in GetItems(itemCode)
			select x.ItemId).FirstOrDefault();
		decimal num2 = Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)Itemid && x.StoreID == (int?)storeid).Sum((ItemQuantity x) => x.OpeningBalance));
		decimal num3 = Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)Itemid && x.StoreID == (int?)storeid).Sum((ItemQuantity x) => x.CurrentBalance));
		num = num2 + num3;
		if (unit == "المتوسطة")
		{
			num /= Convert.ToDecimal((from x in GetItems(itemCode)
				select x.ConvertMediumUnit).FirstOrDefault());
		}
		if (unit == "الكبرى")
		{
			num /= Convert.ToDecimal((from x in GetItems(itemCode)
				select x.ConvertMediumUnit).FirstOrDefault());
			num /= Convert.ToDecimal((from x in GetItems(itemCode)
				select x.ConvertBigUnit).FirstOrDefault());
		}
		return num;
	}

	public int InsertItems(DataTable Items, int Purchese_ID, bool IsWait)
	{
		int num = 0;
		DataTable changes = Items.GetChanges(DataRowState.Added);
		SqlCommand sqlCommand = new SqlCommand("Insert_Order_OrdersDetails", con);
		sqlCommand.CommandType = CommandType.StoredProcedure;
		sqlCommand.Parameters.AddWithValue("@ISWaitng", IsWait);
		SqlParameter sqlParameter = sqlCommand.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Items", changes);
		sqlParameter.SqlDbType = SqlDbType.Structured;
		sqlCommand.CommandTimeout = 999999;
		if (con.State == ConnectionState.Closed)
		{
			con.Open();
		}
		num = sqlCommand.ExecuteNonQuery();
		con.Close();
		return num;
	}

			con.Open();

		con.Close();
		return num;
	}

	public int InsertPurch(out int Code, int SupplierID, string SupplierName, string SupplierPhone, string SupplierVatNum, DateTime Purchases_Date, int PurchType, int CostCenter, string Note, string NoteNum, bool IsWaiting, decimal CostOrder, decimal TotalPrices, decimal Tax, decimal Safy, decimal DiscountNum, decimal DiscountPerantage, decimal Tax_Discount, decimal TotalPrices_Discount, decimal Net, decimal CashMoney, decimal CashBank, bool cashierType, int Roomnum, int TableNum, decimal TobaccoTax, decimal AllTax, int Restaurant_TypeID, string Restaurant_PayType)
	{
		int num = 0;
		Code = 0;
		int iD = Convert.ToInt32(((IQueryable<Order_Orders>)db.Order_Orders).Select((Order_Orders p) => p.ID).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Max()) + 1;
		int num2 = Convert.ToInt32((from p in (IQueryable<Order_Orders>)db.Order_Orders
			where p.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID
			select p.PurBranchID).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Max()) + 1;
		Order_Orders order_Orders = new Order_Orders();
		order_Orders.ID = iD;
		CachCode = num2;
		Code = num2;
		order_Orders.BranchID = LoginDetails.BranchID;
		order_Orders.PurBranchID = num2;
		order_Orders.SupplierID = SupplierID;
		order_Orders.SupplierName = SupplierName;
		order_Orders.SupplierPhone = SupplierPhone;
		order_Orders.SupplierVatNum = SupplierVatNum;
		order_Orders.Purchases_Date = Purchases_Date;
		order_Orders.Order_Paymant_Type = PurchType;
		order_Orders.CostCentersID = CostCenter;
		order_Orders.Note = Note;
		order_Orders.NoteNum = NoteNum;
		order_Orders.IsWaiting = IsWaiting;
		order_Orders.CostOrder = CostOrder;
		order_Orders.TotalPrices = TotalPrices;
		order_Orders.Tax = Tax;
		order_Orders.Safy = Safy;
		order_Orders.DiscountNum = DiscountNum;
		order_Orders.DiscountPerantage = DiscountPerantage;
		order_Orders.Tax_Discount = Tax_Discount;

		order_Orders.RoomNum = Roomnum;
		order_Orders.TableNum = TableNum;
		order_Orders.CashMoney = CashMoney;
		order_Orders.CashBank = CashBank;
		order_Orders.TobaccoTax = TobaccoTax;
		order_Orders.AllTax = AllTax;
		order_Orders.Restaurant_TypeID = Restaurant_TypeID;

		order_Orders.UserID_Add = LoginDetails.UserID;

		order_Orders.UserMacAddress_Add = LoginDetails.macAddress;

	}

	public void deleteItemsByPurcahesId(int PurID)
	{
		List<Order_OrdersDetails> list = ((IQueryable<Order_OrdersDetails>)db.Order_OrdersDetails).Where((Order_OrdersDetails x) => x.Purchese_ID == (int?)PurID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).ToList();
		if (list == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		foreach (Order_OrdersDetails item in list)
		{
			Item_Items item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => (int?)x.ItemId == item.ItemID).FirstOrDefault();
			if (item_Items != null)
			{
				ItemQuantityOut(Convert.ToInt32(item.ItemID), Convert.ToDecimal(item.Quantity), item.ItemUnitType, Convert.ToInt32(item.StoreID), Convert.ToInt32(item_Items.item_Type));
			}
			Order_Orders order_Orders = ((IQueryable<Order_Orders>)db.Order_Orders).Where((Order_Orders z) => z.ID == PurID).FirstOrDefault();
			if (order_Orders.IsWaiting == true)
			{
				ItemQuantityIn(Convert.ToInt32(item.ItemID), Convert.ToDecimal(item.Quantity), item.ItemUnitType, LoginDetails.StoreIDWaiting, 1);
			}
			db.Order_OrdersDetails.Attach(item);
			db.Order_OrdersDetails.Remove(item);
		}
		((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
	}

	public int UpdatePurch(int PurID, int SupplierID, string SupplierName, string SupplierPhone, string SupplierVatNum, DateTime Purchases_Date, int PurchType, int CostCenter, string Note, string NoteNum, bool IsWaiting, decimal CostOrder, decimal TotalPrices, decimal Tax, decimal Safy, decimal DiscountNum, decimal DiscountPerantage, decimal Tax_Discount, decimal TotalPrices_Discount, decimal Net, decimal CashMoney, decimal CashBank, bool cashierType, int Roomnum, int TableNum, decimal TobaccoTax, decimal AllTax)
	{
		int result = 0;
		Order_Orders order_Orders = ((IQueryable<Order_Orders>)db.Order_Orders).Where((Order_Orders x) => x.PurBranchID == (int?)PurID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault();
		int iD = ((IQueryable<Order_Orders>)db.Order_Orders).Where((Order_Orders x) => x.PurBranchID == (int?)PurID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault().ID;
		if (order_Orders != null)
		{
			order_Orders.SupplierID = SupplierID;
			order_Orders.SupplierName = SupplierName;
			order_Orders.SupplierPhone = SupplierPhone;
			order_Orders.SupplierVatNum = SupplierVatNum;
			order_Orders.Purchases_Date = Purchases_Date;
			order_Orders.Order_Paymant_Type = PurchType;
			order_Orders.CostCentersID = CostCenter;
			order_Orders.Note = Note;
			order_Orders.NoteNum = NoteNum;
			order_Orders.TotalPrices = TotalPrices;
			order_Orders.Tax = Tax;
			order_Orders.Safy = Safy;
			order_Orders.IsWaiting = IsWaiting;
			order_Orders.CostOrder = CostOrder;
			order_Orders.DiscountNum = DiscountNum;
			order_Orders.DiscountPerantage = DiscountPerantage;
			order_Orders.Tax_Discount = Tax_Discount;
			order_Orders.TotalPrices_Discount = TotalPrices_Discount;
			order_Orders.Net = Net;
			order_Orders.CashMoney = CashMoney;
			order_Orders.CashBank = CashBank;
			order_Orders.OrderCashierType = cashierType;
			order_Orders.RoomNum = Roomnum;
			order_Orders.TableNum = TableNum;
			order_Orders.TobaccoTax = TobaccoTax;
			order_Orders.AllTax = AllTax;
			order_Orders.UserID_Update = LoginDetails.UserID;
			order_Orders.UserBranch_Update = LoginDetails.GroupID;
			order_Orders.UserMacAddress_Update = LoginDetails.macAddress;
			order_Orders.UserDate_Update = DateTime.Now;

	}

	public int Delete(int PurID)
	{
		int result = 0;
		Order_Orders order_Orders = ((IQueryable<Order_Orders>)db.Order_Orders).Where((Order_Orders x) => x.PurBranchID == (int?)PurID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault();
		if (order_Orders != null)
		{
			deleteItemsByPurcahesId(PurID);
			db.Order_Orders.Attach(order_Orders);
			db.Order_Orders.Remove(order_Orders);
			result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
		}
		return result;
	}

	public int InsertTran(out int TRNIDDS, int PurID, int PurType, string Name, string NoteNum, DateTime PurDate)
	{
		int num = 0;
		TRNIDDS = 0;
		int num2 = Convert.ToInt32(((IQueryable<Tran_Tran>)db.Tran_Tran).Select((Tran_Tran p) => p.ID).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Max()) + 1;
		Tran_Tran tran_Tran = new Tran_Tran();
		tran_Tran.ID = num2;
		tran_Tran.ReferenceCode = PurID;
		tran_Tran.TranTypeID = 5;
		tran_Tran.DocCode = NoteNum;
		tran_Tran.TranDate = PurDate;
		tran_Tran.BranchID = LoginDetails.BranchID;
		if (PurType == 2)
		{
			tran_Tran.Note = " مبيعات أجلة  رقم  ( " + PurID + " ) ح /  " + Name;
		}
		else
		{
			tran_Tran.Note = " مبيعات نقدية  رقم ( " + PurID + " )";
		}
		tran_Tran.UserID_Add = LoginDetails.UserID;
		tran_Tran.UserBranch_Add = LoginDetails.BranchID;

	}

	public int UpdateTran(out int TRNIDDS, int PurID, int PurType, string Name, string NoteNum, DateTime PurDate)
	{
		int result = 0;
		TRNIDDS = 0;
		Tran_Tran tran_Tran = ((IQueryable<Tran_Tran>)db.Tran_Tran).Where((Tran_Tran x) => x.ReferenceCode == (int?)PurID && x.TranTypeID == (int?)5 && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault();
		if (tran_Tran != null)
		{
			tran_Tran.DocCode = NoteNum;
			tran_Tran.TranDate = PurDate;
			tran_Tran.BranchID = LoginDetails.BranchID;
			if (PurType == 2)
			{
				tran_Tran.Note = " مبيعات أجلة  رقم  ( " + PurID + " ) ح /  " + Name + " معدلة ";
			}
			else
			{
				tran_Tran.Note = " مبيعات نقدية  رقم ( " + PurID + " ) معدلة ";
			}
			tran_Tran.UserID_Update = LoginDetails.UserID;
			tran_Tran.UserBranch_Update = LoginDetails.GroupID;
			tran_Tran.UserMacAddress_Update = LoginDetails.macAddress;
			tran_Tran.UserDate_Update = DateTime.Now;
			TRNIDDS = Convert.ToInt32(tran_Tran.ID);
			result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
		}
		return result;
	}

	public int DeleteTran(int Code)
	{
		int result = 0;
		try
		{
			int TranID = ((IQueryable<Tran_Tran>)db.Tran_Tran).Where((Tran_Tran x) => x.ReferenceCode == (int?)Code && x.TranTypeID == (int?)5 && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault().ID;
			Tran_Tran tran_Tran = ((IQueryable<Tran_Tran>)db.Tran_Tran).Where((Tran_Tran x) => x.ID == TranID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault();
			if (tran_Tran != null)
			{
				db.Tran_Tran.Attach(tran_Tran);
				db.Tran_Tran.Remove(tran_Tran);
				result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
		return result;
	}

	public int DeleteTranDetails(int Code)
	{
		int result = 0;
		try
		{
			int TranID = ((IQueryable<Tran_Tran>)db.Tran_Tran).Where((Tran_Tran x) => x.ReferenceCode == (int?)Code && x.TranTypeID == (int?)5 && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault().ID;
			List<Tran_TranDetails> list = ((IQueryable<Tran_TranDetails>)db.Tran_TranDetails).Where((Tran_TranDetails x) => x.TranSn == (int?)TranID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).ToList();
			if (list != null)
			{
				foreach (Tran_TranDetails item in list)
				{
					db.Tran_TranDetails.Attach(item);
					db.Tran_TranDetails.Remove(item);
				}
				result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
		return result;
	}

	public int GetItmID(string Code)
	{
		return (from xx in (IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items
			where xx.Item_code == Code
			select xx.ItemId).FirstOrDefault();
	}

	public decimal GetSellPermission(string Item_code, int UserSellPricePer, string Type)
	{
		decimal result = 0m;
		try
		{
			switch (Type)
			{
			case "الصغرى":
				if (UserSellPricePer == 1)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().SellPriceSmall);
				}
				if (UserSellPricePer == 2)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().SellPriceSmall2);
				}
				if (UserSellPricePer == 3)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().SellPriceSmall3);
				}
				if (UserSellPricePer >= 4)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().LastCost);
				}
				break;
			case "المتوسطة":
				if (UserSellPricePer == 1)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().SellPriceMedium);
				}
				if (UserSellPricePer == 2)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().SellPriceMedium2);
				}
				if (UserSellPricePer == 3)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().SellPriceMedium3);
				}
				if (UserSellPricePer >= 4)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().LastCost2);
				}
				break;
			case "الكبرى":
				if (UserSellPricePer == 1)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().SellpriceLarge);
				}
				if (UserSellPricePer == 2)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().SellpriceLarge2);
				}
				if (UserSellPricePer == 3)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().SellpriceLarge3);
				}
				if (UserSellPricePer >= 4)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().LastCost3);
				}
				break;
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			result = 0m;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public decimal GetQuantityPermissionReturn(int ItemID, int PurID)
	{
		decimal result = 0m;
		Order_OrdersDetails order_OrdersDetails = ((IQueryable<Order_OrdersDetails>)db.Order_OrdersDetails).Where((Order_OrdersDetails x) => x.Purchese_ID == (int?)PurID && x.ItemID == (int?)ItemID).FirstOrDefault();
		if (order_OrdersDetails != null)
		{
			return Convert.ToDecimal(order_OrdersDetails.Quantity);
		}
		return result;
	}

	public decimal GetQuantityPermission(int ItemID, int StoreID, string Type)
	{
		decimal result = 0m;
		try
		{
			switch (Type)
			{
			case "الصغرى":
			{
				ItemQuantity itemQuantity2 = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)ItemID && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
				if (itemQuantity2 != null)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance);
				}
				break;
			}
			case "المتوسطة":
			{
				ItemQuantity itemQuantity3 = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)ItemID && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
				if (itemQuantity3 != null)
				{
					decimal num4 = Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.ItemId == ItemID).FirstOrDefault().ConvertMediumUnit);
					decimal num5 = Convert.ToDecimal(itemQuantity3.CurrentBalance);
					return num5 / num4;
				}
				break;
			}
			case "الكبرى":
			{
				ItemQuantity itemQuantity = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)ItemID && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
				if (itemQuantity != null)
				{
					Item_Items item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.ItemId == ItemID).FirstOrDefault();
					decimal num = Convert.ToDecimal(item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit);
					decimal num2 = Convert.ToDecimal(item_Items.ConvertBigUnit);
					decimal num3 = Convert.ToDecimal(itemQuantity.CurrentBalance);
					return num3 / (num * num2);
				}
				break;
			}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			result = 0m;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public int UpdateRoom(int RoomID, bool ISPause, decimal Money, int OrderID)
	{
		int result = 0;
		Restaurant_Room restaurant_Room = ((IQueryable<Restaurant_Room>)db.Restaurant_Room).Where((Restaurant_Room x) => x.ID == RoomID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault();
		if (restaurant_Room != null)
		{
			restaurant_Room.ISPause = ISPause;
			restaurant_Room.Money = Money;
			restaurant_Room.OrderID = OrderID;
			restaurant_Room.BranchID = LoginDetails.BranchID;
			result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
		}
		return result;
	}

	public void updateOdrderMahlyType(int OdederID, decimal Cash, decimal Bank, decimal Discount)
	{
		int TRNIDDS = 0;
		Order_Orders order_Orders = ((IQueryable<Order_Orders>)db.Order_Orders).Where((Order_Orders x) => x.PurBranchID == (int?)OdederID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault();
		decimal value = Convert.ToDecimal(order_Orders.CashMoney) + Cash;
		decimal value2 = Convert.ToDecimal(order_Orders.CashBank) + Bank;
		if (order_Orders != null)
		{
			order_Orders.Order_Paymant_Type = 1;
			order_Orders.CashMoney = value;
			order_Orders.CashBank = value2;
			((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
		}
		Tran_Tran tran_Tran = ((IQueryable<Tran_Tran>)db.Tran_Tran).Where((Tran_Tran x) => x.ReferenceCode == (int?)OdederID && x.TranTypeID == (int?)5 && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault();
		if (tran_Tran != null)
		{
			DeleteTranDetails(OdederID);
			DeleteTran(OdederID);
		}
		InsertTran(out TRNIDDS, OdederID, Convert.ToInt32(order_Orders.Order_Paymant_Type), order_Orders.SupplierName, order_Orders.NoteNum, Convert.ToDateTime(order_Orders.Purchases_Date));
		InsertTranDetailsCash(TRNIDDS, OdederID, Convert.ToDecimal(order_Orders.CostOrder), Convert.ToDecimal(order_Orders.TotalPrices_Discount), Convert.ToDecimal(order_Orders.TobaccoTax), Convert.ToDecimal(order_Orders.AllTax), Convert.ToDecimal(Cash), Convert.ToDecimal(Bank), Convert.ToDecimal(order_Orders.Net), 0);
		if (Discount > 0m)
		{
			InsertTranDetailsDiscount(TRNIDDS, OdederID, Convert.ToDecimal(order_Orders.CostOrder), Convert.ToDecimal(order_Orders.TotalPrices_Discount), Convert.ToDecimal(order_Orders.TobaccoTax), Convert.ToDecimal(order_Orders.AllTax), Convert.ToDecimal(Cash), Convert.ToDecimal(Bank), Convert.ToDecimal(order_Orders.Net), 0, Discount);
		}
	}

	public void UpdateOrderDetialsIsPrint(int ORDID)

	}

	public int InsertTranDetailsDiscount(int TranID, int PurID, decimal TotalCost, decimal TotalSell, decimal TotalTobacc, decimal Vat, decimal TotalCash, decimal TotalBank, decimal NetCash, int NoteNum, decimal Discount)

		int num = 0;

		tran_TranDetails.TranSn = TranID;
		tran_TranDetails.Account_Sn = LoginDetails.Acc_DiscountForAll;

		tran_TranDetails.TranDesc = "  خصم مسموح به لفاتورة رقم  ( " + PurID + " ) ";

		tran_TranDetails.Credit = 0m;

	}

	public int SaveOrders(int SupplierID, bool IsWaiting, string SupplierName, string SupplierPhone, string SupplierVatNum, DateTime Purchases_Date, int Order_Paymant_Type, string Restaurant_PayType, int Restaurant_TypeID, int CostCentersID, int BounceID, string Note, string NoteNum, decimal CostOrder, decimal Tax, decimal TotalPrices, decimal Safy, decimal DiscountNum, decimal DiscountPerantage, decimal Tax_Discount, decimal TotalPrices_Discount, decimal TobaccoTax, decimal AllTax, decimal Net, decimal CashMoney, decimal CashBank, bool OrderCashierType, int RoomNum, int TableNum, DataTable Items, int Sectoral, string SalesMan, DateTime DateHold, string BuildingNum, string Street, string District, string City, string Country, string PostalCode, string AdditionalNum, string CommercialRecord, decimal HasmPer, decimal HasmAmount, decimal Charge, int ProjectID, decimal AllDiscount, decimal BounsAmount, string DisCode, decimal AmountPaid, decimal Rest, string CarName, string CarModel, string PlateNumber, string ChassisNum, string CarColor, string Counter)
	{
		int result = 0;
		if (!OrderCashierType)
		{
			Restaurant_TypeID = 1;
		}
		else if (OrderCashierType)
		{
			Restaurant_TypeID = 2;
		}
		using (SqlCommand sqlCommand = new SqlCommand("Insert_Order_Order_ALL", con))
		{
			sqlCommand.CommandType = CommandType.StoredProcedure;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Clear();
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@BranchID", SqlDbType.Int).Value = LoginDetails.BranchID;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@SupplierID", SqlDbType.Int).Value = SupplierID;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@IsWaiting", SqlDbType.Bit).Value = IsWaiting;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@SupplierName", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = SupplierName;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@SupplierPhone", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = SupplierPhone;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@BuildingNum", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = BuildingNum;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Street", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = Street;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@District", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = District;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@City", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = City;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Country", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = Country;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@PostalCode", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = PostalCode;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@AdditionalNum", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = AdditionalNum;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@CommercialRecord", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = CommercialRecord;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@SupplierVatNum", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = SupplierVatNum;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Purchases_Date", SqlDbType.DateTime).Value = Purchases_Date;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Order_Paymant_Type", SqlDbType.Int).Value = Order_Paymant_Type;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Restaurant_PayType", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = Restaurant_PayType;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Restaurant_TypeID", SqlDbType.Int).Value = Restaurant_TypeID;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@CostCentersID", SqlDbType.Int).Value = CostCentersID;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@BounceID", SqlDbType.Int).Value = BounceID;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Note", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = Note;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@NoteNum", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = NoteNum;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@CostOrder", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = CostOrder;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Tax", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = Tax;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@TotalPrices", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = TotalPrices;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Safy", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = Safy;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@DiscountNum", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = DiscountNum;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@DiscountPerantage", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = DiscountPerantage;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@HasmPer", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = HasmPer;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@HasmAmount", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = HasmAmount;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Tax_Discount", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = Tax_Discount;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@TotalPrices_Discount", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = TotalPrices_Discount;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@TobaccoTax", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = TobaccoTax;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@AllTax", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = AllTax;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Net", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = Net;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@CashMoney", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = CashMoney;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@CashBank", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = CashBank;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@OrderCashierType", SqlDbType.Bit).Value = OrderCashierType;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@ProjectID", SqlDbType.Int).Value = ProjectID;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@RoomNum", SqlDbType.Int).Value = RoomNum;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@TableNum", SqlDbType.Int).Value = TableNum;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Sectoral", SqlDbType.Int).Value = Sectoral;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@UserID_Add", SqlDbType.Int).Value = LoginDetails.UserID;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@UserBranch_Add", SqlDbType.Int).Value = LoginDetails.BranchID;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@UserMacAddress_Add", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = LoginDetails.macAddress;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@SalesMan", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = SalesMan;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@DateHold", SqlDbType.DateTime).Value = DateHold;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Charge", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = Charge;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@AllDiscount", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = AllDiscount;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@BounsAmount", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = BounsAmount;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@DisCode", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = DisCode;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@AmountPaid", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = AmountPaid;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Rest", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = Rest;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@CarName", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = CarName;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@CarModel", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = CarModel;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@PlateNumber", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = PlateNumber;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@ChassisNum", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = ChassisNum;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@CarColor", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = CarColor;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Counter", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = Counter;
			DataTable changes = Items.GetChanges(DataRowState.Added);
			sqlCommand.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Items", changes);
			SqlParameter sqlParameter = sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Return Value", SqlDbType.Int);
			sqlParameter.Direction = ParameterDirection.ReturnValue;
			sqlCommand.CommandTimeout = 999999;
			if (con.State == ConnectionState.Closed)
			{
				con.Open();
			}
			try
			{
				result = sqlCommand.ExecuteNonQuery();
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			object value = sqlParameter.Value;
			con.Close();
			result = Convert.ToInt32(value);
		}
		return result;
	}

	public int SaveOrdersDraft(int SupplierID, bool IsWaiting, string SupplierName, string SupplierPhone, string SupplierVatNum, DateTime Purchases_Date, int Order_Paymant_Type, string Restaurant_PayType, int Restaurant_TypeID, int CostCentersID, int BounceID, string Note, string NoteNum, decimal CostOrder, decimal Tax, decimal TotalPrices, decimal Safy, decimal DiscountNum, decimal DiscountPerantage, decimal Tax_Discount, decimal TotalPrices_Discount, decimal TobaccoTax, decimal AllTax, decimal Net, decimal CashMoney, decimal CashBank, bool OrderCashierType, int RoomNum, int TableNum, DataTable Items, int Sectoral, string SalesMan, DateTime DateHold, string BuildingNum, string Street, string District, string City, string Country, string PostalCode, string AdditionalNum, string CommercialRecord, decimal HasmPer, decimal HasmAmount, decimal Charge, int ProjectID, decimal AllDiscount, decimal BounsAmount, string DisCode, decimal AmountPaid, decimal Rest, string CarName, string CarModel, string PlateNumber, string ChassisNum, string CarColor, string Counter)
	{

		{
			Restaurant_TypeID = 1;
		}
		else if (OrderCashierType)
		{
			Restaurant_TypeID = 2;
		}
		using (SqlCommand sqlCommand = new SqlCommand("Insert_Order_Order_ALL_Draft", con))
		{
			sqlCommand.CommandType = CommandType.StoredProcedure;

			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@SupplierID", SqlDbType.Int).Value = SupplierID;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@IsWaiting", SqlDbType.Bit).Value = IsWaiting;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@SupplierName", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = SupplierName;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@SupplierPhone", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = SupplierPhone;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@BuildingNum", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = BuildingNum;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Street", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = Street;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@District", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = District;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@City", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = City;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Country", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = Country;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@PostalCode", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = PostalCode;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@AdditionalNum", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = AdditionalNum;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@CommercialRecord", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = CommercialRecord;

	public int InsertRes(int OrderID, int HallsID, DateTime BookingDate, DateTime InstallationDate, DateTime jawDate)
	{
		int num = 0;
		int num2 = Convert.ToInt32(((IQueryable<Order_Reservations>)db.Order_Reservations).Select((Order_Reservations p) => p.ID).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Max()) + 1;
		Order_Reservations order_Reservations = new Order_Reservations();
		order_Reservations.ID = num2;
		order_Reservations.OrderID = OrderID;
		order_Reservations.HallsID = HallsID;
		order_Reservations.BookingDate = BookingDate;
		order_Reservations.InstallationDate = InstallationDate;
		order_Reservations.jawDate = jawDate;
		order_Reservations.BranchID = LoginDetails.BranchID;
		order_Reservations.UserID_Add = LoginDetails.UserID;
		order_Reservations.UserBranch_Add = LoginDetails.BranchID;
		order_Reservations.UserMacAddress_Add = LoginDetails.macAddress;
		order_Reservations.UserDate_Add = DateTime.Now;
		db.Order_Reservations.Add(order_Reservations);
		num = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
		return num2;
	}

	public int UpdateRes(int OrderID, int HallsID, DateTime BookingDate, DateTime InstallationDate, DateTime jawDate)
	{
		int result = 0;
		Order_Reservations order_Reservations = ((IQueryable<Order_Reservations>)db.Order_Reservations).Where((Order_Reservations x) => x.OrderID == (int?)OrderID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault();
		if (order_Reservations != null)
		{
			order_Reservations.HallsID = HallsID;
			order_Reservations.BookingDate = BookingDate;
			order_Reservations.InstallationDate = InstallationDate;
			order_Reservations.jawDate = jawDate;
			order_Reservations.UserID_Add = LoginDetails.UserID;
			order_Reservations.UserBranch_Add = LoginDetails.BranchID;
			order_Reservations.UserMacAddress_Add = LoginDetails.macAddress;
			order_Reservations.UserDate_Add = DateTime.Now;
			((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
			result = OrderID;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public int DeleteRes(int ID)
	{
		int result = 0;
		Order_Reservations order_Reservations = ((IQueryable<Order_Reservations>)db.Order_Reservations).Where((Order_Reservations x) => x.OrderID == (int?)ID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault();
		if (order_Reservations != null)
		{
			db.Order_Reservations.Attach(order_Reservations);
			db.Order_Reservations.Remove(order_Reservations);
			result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
		}
		return result;
	}

	public void UpdateOrderAFterElctorncFatora(int orderId, string newQRCode, string OrderTypeId, string NormalOrSimpleInvoice, bool ThirdParty, bool NominalInvoice, bool ExportInvoice, bool SummaryInvoice, bool SelfInvoice)
	{
		Order_Orders order_Orders = ((IQueryable<Order_Orders>)db.Order_Orders).FirstOrDefault((Order_Orders x) => x.ID == orderId);
		if (order_Orders != null)
		{
			order_Orders.QRCode = newQRCode;
			order_Orders.OrderTypeElectronicInvoiceId = OrderTypeId;
			order_Orders.NormalOrSimpleInvoice = NormalOrSimpleInvoice;
			order_Orders.ThirdParty = ThirdParty;
			order_Orders.NominalInvoice = NominalInvoice;
			order_Orders.ExportInvoice = ExportInvoice;
			order_Orders.SummaryInvoice = SummaryInvoice;
			order_Orders.SelfInvoice = SelfInvoice;
			((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
		}
	}

	public void MangeGlassess(int orderId, string RightSph, string RightCYL, string RightAxis, string RightAdd, string LeftSph, string LeftCYL, string LeftAxis, string LeftAdd, string NoteGlass)
	{
		Order_Extension order_Extension = ((IQueryable<Order_Extension>)db.Order_Extension).FirstOrDefault((Order_Extension o) => o.OrderID == orderId);
		if (order_Extension != null)
		{
			order_Extension.RightSph = RightSph;
			order_Extension.RightCYL = RightCYL;
			order_Extension.RightAxis = RightAxis;
			order_Extension.RightAdd = RightAdd;
			order_Extension.LeftSph = LeftSph;
			order_Extension.LeftCYL = LeftCYL;
			order_Extension.LeftAxis = LeftAxis;
			order_Extension.LeftAdd = LeftAdd;
			order_Extension.Note = NoteGlass;
		}
		else
		{
			Order_Extension order_Extension2 = new Order_Extension
			{
				OrderID = orderId,
				RightSph = RightSph,
				RightCYL = RightCYL,
				RightAxis = RightAxis,
				RightAdd = RightAdd,
				LeftSph = LeftSph,
				LeftCYL = LeftCYL,
				LeftAxis = LeftAxis,
				LeftAdd = LeftAdd,
				Note = NoteGlass
			};
			db.Order_Extension.Add(order_Extension2);
		}
		((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
	}

	public Order_Extension GetGlassess(int orderId)
	{
		return ((IQueryable<Order_Extension>)db.Order_Extension).FirstOrDefault((Order_Extension o) => o.OrderID == orderId);
	}

	public void DeleteGlassess(int orderId)
	{
		Order_Extension order_Extension = ((IQueryable<Order_Extension>)db.Order_Extension).FirstOrDefault((Order_Extension o) => o.OrderID == orderId);
		if (order_Extension != null)
		{
			db.Order_Extension.Remove(order_Extension);
			((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
		}
	}

	public void UpdateDraftNum(int id)
	{
		Order_OrdersDraft order_OrdersDraft = ((IQueryable<Order_OrdersDraft>)db.Order_OrdersDraft).FirstOrDefault((Order_OrdersDraft d) => d.ID == id);
		if (order_OrdersDraft != null)
		{
			int valueOrDefault = order_OrdersDraft.OrderNum.GetValueOrDefault();
			order_OrdersDraft.OrderNum = valueOrDefault + 1;
			((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
		}
	}
}

using System;

namespace GTSErpSystem.BLL.Orders;

public class Class_OrdersReturn
{
	public class MyObject
	{
		public string ItemId { get; set; }

		public int ItemUnitID { get; set; }

		public decimal LastPriceSmall { get; set; }

		public decimal SmallUnitPrice { get; set; }

		public decimal Discount { get; set; }

		public decimal Quantity { get; set; }

		public int StoreID { get; set; }

		public decimal UnitPrice { get; set; }

		public decimal TotalPrice { get; set; }

		public decimal NetUnitPrice { get; set; }

		public decimal NetTotalPrice { get; set; }

		public decimal VAT { get; set; }

		public decimal VAT_discount { get; set; }

		public string UnitType { get; set; }
	}

	private int CachCode = 0;

	private DataTable dtCurrentTable = new DataTable();

	private GTSdbEntities db = new GTSdbEntities();

	private int DecimalNum = LoginDetails.DecimalNum;

	private DataTable AvergItemTable = new DataTable();

	public List<Account_AccountType> GetAccountType()
	{
		return ((IEnumerable<Account_AccountType>)db.Account_AccountType).ToList();
	}

	}

	public List<Order_OrderReturn> GetPurchases()

	}

	public List<Order_OrderReturn> GetPurchases(int Code)

	}

	public int GetStoreIDByName(string Name)

			where a.Store_Name == Name
