	public int InsertItems(DataTable Items, int Purchese_ID, int FromPurchID)
	{
		int result = 0;
		foreach (DataRow row in Items.Rows)
		{
			if (!(row["كود_الصنف"].ToString() != ""))
			{
				continue;
			}
			Order_CheckoutOrdersDetails order_CheckoutOrdersDetails = new Order_CheckoutOrdersDetails();
			string Itemcod = row["كود_الصنف"].ToString();
			string value = Convert.ToString(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Itemcod).FirstOrDefault().ItemId);
			int ItemRowChek = Convert.ToInt32(value);
			Item_Items item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items z) => z.ItemId == ItemRowChek).FirstOrDefault();
			if (item_Items != null)
			{
				order_CheckoutOrdersDetails.Purchese_ID = Purchese_ID;
				order_CheckoutOrdersDetails.FromPurchID = FromPurchID;
				order_CheckoutOrdersDetails.BranchID = LoginDetails.BranchID;
				order_CheckoutOrdersDetails.ItemID = Convert.ToInt32(item_Items.ItemId);
				string nmae = row["الوحدة"].ToString();
				order_CheckoutOrdersDetails.ItemUnitID = GetUnitIdByName(nmae);
				order_CheckoutOrdersDetails.Quantity = Convert.ToDecimal(row["الكمية"].ToString());
				order_CheckoutOrdersDetails.ItemUnitType = row["أسم_الوحدة"].ToString();
				order_CheckoutOrdersDetails.StoreID = Convert.ToInt32(row["رقم المخزن"]);
				order_CheckoutOrdersDetails.IsWaiting = true;
				db.Order_CheckoutOrdersDetails.Add(order_CheckoutOrdersDetails);
				result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
				if (item_Items.item_Type == 1)
				{
					ItemQuantityOut(Convert.ToInt32(value), Convert.ToDecimal(row["الكمية"].ToString()), row["أسم_الوحدة"].ToString(), Convert.ToInt32(row["رقم المخزن"].ToString()), 1);
				}
				if (item_Items.item_Type == 3)
				{
					ItemQuantityOut(Convert.ToInt32(value), Convert.ToDecimal(row["الكمية"].ToString()), row["أسم_الوحدة"].ToString(), Convert.ToInt32(row["رقم المخزن"].ToString()), 1);
				}
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	public int InsertPurch(out int Code, int FromPurchID, int SupplierID, string SupplierName, string SupplierPhone, string SupplierVatNum, DateTime Purchases_Date, int PurchType, int CostCenter, string Note, string NoteNum, bool ISWait, decimal NetOrder, decimal CashOrder, decimal BankOrder, decimal CashCheckOut, decimal BankCheckOut)
	{
		int num = 0;
		Code = 0;
		int iD = Convert.ToInt32(((IQueryable<Order_CheckoutOrders>)db.Order_CheckoutOrders).Select((Order_CheckoutOrders p) => p.ID).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Max()) + 1;
		int num2 = Convert.ToInt32((from p in (IQueryable<Order_CheckoutOrders>)db.Order_CheckoutOrders
			where p.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID
			select p.PurBranchID).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Max()) + 1;
		Order_CheckoutOrders order_CheckoutOrders = new Order_CheckoutOrders();
		order_CheckoutOrders.ID = iD;
		CachCode = num2;
		Code = num2;
		order_CheckoutOrders.FromPurchesID = FromPurchID;
		order_CheckoutOrders.BranchID = LoginDetails.BranchID;
		order_CheckoutOrders.PurBranchID = num2;
		order_CheckoutOrders.SupplierID = SupplierID;
		order_CheckoutOrders.SupplierName = SupplierName;
		order_CheckoutOrders.SupplierPhone = SupplierPhone;
		order_CheckoutOrders.SupplierVatNum = SupplierVatNum;
		order_CheckoutOrders.Purchases_Date = Purchases_Date;
		order_CheckoutOrders.Order_Paymant_Type = PurchType;
		order_CheckoutOrders.CostCentersID = CostCenter;
		order_CheckoutOrders.Note = Note;
		order_CheckoutOrders.IsWaiting = ISWait;
		order_CheckoutOrders.UserID_Add = LoginDetails.UserID;
		order_CheckoutOrders.UserBranch_Add = LoginDetails.BranchID;
		order_CheckoutOrders.UserMacAddress_Add = LoginDetails.macAddress;
		order_CheckoutOrders.UserDate_Add = DateTime.Now;
		order_CheckoutOrders.NetOrder = NetOrder;
		order_CheckoutOrders.CashOrder = CashOrder;
		order_CheckoutOrders.BankOrder = BankOrder;
		order_CheckoutOrders.CashCheckOut = CashCheckOut;
		order_CheckoutOrders.BankCheckOut = BankCheckOut;
		order_CheckoutOrders.ProjectId = 1;
		order_CheckoutOrders.YearId = 1;
		db.Order_CheckoutOrders.Add(order_CheckoutOrders);
		num = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
		return num2;
	}

	public void deleteItemsByPurcahesId(int PurID)
	{
		List<Order_CheckoutOrdersDetails> list = ((IQueryable<Order_CheckoutOrdersDetails>)db.Order_CheckoutOrdersDetails).Where((Order_CheckoutOrdersDetails x) => x.Purchese_ID == (int?)PurID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).ToList();
		if (list == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		foreach (Order_CheckoutOrdersDetails item in list)
		{
			Item_Items item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => (int?)x.ItemId == item.ItemID).FirstOrDefault();
			ItemQuantityIN(Convert.ToInt32(item.ItemID), Convert.ToDecimal(item.Quantity), item.ItemUnitType, Convert.ToInt32(item.StoreID), 1);
			db.Order_CheckoutOrdersDetails.Attach(item);
			db.Order_CheckoutOrdersDetails.Remove(item);
		}
		((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
	}

	public int UpdatePurch(int PurID, int SupplierID, string SupplierName, string SupplierPhone, string SupplierVatNum, DateTime Purchases_Date, int PurchType, int CostCenter, string Note, string NoteNum, bool ISWait, decimal NetOrder, decimal CashOrder, decimal BankOrder, decimal CashCheckOut, decimal BankCheckOut)
	{
		int result = 0;
		Order_CheckoutOrders order_CheckoutOrders = ((IQueryable<Order_CheckoutOrders>)db.Order_CheckoutOrders).Where((Order_CheckoutOrders x) => x.PurBranchID == (int?)PurID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault();
		int iD = ((IQueryable<Order_CheckoutOrders>)db.Order_CheckoutOrders).Where((Order_CheckoutOrders x) => x.PurBranchID == (int?)PurID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault().ID;
		if (order_CheckoutOrders != null)
		{
			order_CheckoutOrders.SupplierID = SupplierID;
			order_CheckoutOrders.SupplierName = SupplierName;
			order_CheckoutOrders.SupplierPhone = SupplierPhone;
			order_CheckoutOrders.SupplierVatNum = SupplierVatNum;
			order_CheckoutOrders.Purchases_Date = Purchases_Date;
			order_CheckoutOrders.Order_Paymant_Type = PurchType;
			order_CheckoutOrders.CostCentersID = CostCenter;
			order_CheckoutOrders.Note = Note;
			order_CheckoutOrders.NoteNum = NoteNum;
			order_CheckoutOrders.IsWaiting = ISWait;
			order_CheckoutOrders.UserID_Update = LoginDetails.UserID;
			order_CheckoutOrders.UserBranch_Update = LoginDetails.GroupID;
			order_CheckoutOrders.UserMacAddress_Update = LoginDetails.macAddress;
			order_CheckoutOrders.UserDate_Update = DateTime.Now;
			order_CheckoutOrders.NetOrder = NetOrder;
			order_CheckoutOrders.CashOrder = CashOrder;
			order_CheckoutOrders.BankOrder = BankOrder;
			order_CheckoutOrders.CashCheckOut = CashCheckOut;
			order_CheckoutOrders.BankCheckOut = BankCheckOut;
			((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
			result = PurID;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public int Delete(int PurID)
	{
		int result = 0;
		Order_CheckoutOrders order_CheckoutOrders = ((IQueryable<Order_CheckoutOrders>)db.Order_CheckoutOrders).Where((Order_CheckoutOrders x) => x.PurBranchID == (int?)PurID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault();
		if (order_CheckoutOrders != null)
		{
			deleteItemsByPurcahesId(PurID);
			db.Order_CheckoutOrders.Attach(order_CheckoutOrders);
			db.Order_CheckoutOrders.Remove(order_CheckoutOrders);
			result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
		}
		return result;
	}

	public int InsertItemsTran(int TranSn, int Account_Cust, int Account_CashAcct, int Account_BankAcct, decimal FrmBank, decimal FrmCash, int CostCentersID)
	{
		int num = 0;
		Tran_TranDetails tran_TranDetails = new Tran_TranDetails();
		tran_TranDetails.TranSn = TranSn;
		tran_TranDetails.Account_Sn = Account_Cust;
		tran_TranDetails.AccounIindex = 1;
		tran_TranDetails.Debit = 0m;
		tran_TranDetails.Credit = FrmBank + FrmCash;
		tran_TranDetails.CostCentersID = 0;
		tran_TranDetails.BranchID = LoginDetails.BranchID;
		tran_TranDetails.TranDesc = "صرف حجز البضاعه";
		db.Tran_TranDetails.Add(tran_TranDetails);
		num = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
		if (FrmBank > 0m)
		{
			Tran_TranDetails tran_TranDetails2 = new Tran_TranDetails();
			tran_TranDetails2.TranSn = TranSn;
			tran_TranDetails2.Account_Sn = Account_BankAcct;
			tran_TranDetails2.AccounIindex = 2;
			tran_TranDetails2.Debit = FrmBank;
			tran_TranDetails2.Credit = 0m;
			tran_TranDetails2.CostCentersID = 0;
			tran_TranDetails2.BranchID = LoginDetails.BranchID;
			tran_TranDetails2.TranDesc = "صرف حجز البضاعه";
			db.Tran_TranDetails.Add(tran_TranDetails2);
			((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
		}
		if (FrmCash > 0m)
		{
			Tran_TranDetails tran_TranDetails3 = new Tran_TranDetails();
			tran_TranDetails3.TranSn = TranSn;
			tran_TranDetails3.Account_Sn = Account_CashAcct;
			tran_TranDetails3.AccounIindex = 3;
			tran_TranDetails3.Debit = FrmCash;
			tran_TranDetails3.Credit = 0m;
			tran_TranDetails3.CostCentersID = 0;
			tran_TranDetails3.BranchID = LoginDetails.BranchID;
			tran_TranDetails3.TranDesc = "صرف حجز البضاعه";
			db.Tran_TranDetails.Add(tran_TranDetails3);
			((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
		}
		return num;
	}

	public int UpdateTran(int TRNCode, string NoteNum, DateTime PurDate, string Note, int TranTypeID)
	{
		int result = 0;
		Tran_Tran tran_Tran = ((IQueryable<Tran_Tran>)db.Tran_Tran).Where((Tran_Tran x) => x.ReferenceCode == (int?)TRNCode && x.TranTypeID == (int?)TranTypeID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault();
		if (tran_Tran != null)
		{
			tran_Tran.DocCode = NoteNum;
			tran_Tran.TranDate = PurDate;
			tran_Tran.BranchID = LoginDetails.BranchID;
			tran_Tran.Note = Note;
			tran_Tran.UserID_Update = LoginDetails.UserID;
			tran_Tran.UserBranch_Update = LoginDetails.GroupID;
			tran_Tran.UserMacAddress_Update = LoginDetails.macAddress;
			tran_Tran.UserDate_Update = DateTime.Now;
			((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
			result = tran_Tran.ID;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public int DeleteTran(int Code, int TranTypeID)
	{
		int result = 0;
		int TranID = ((IQueryable<Tran_Tran>)db.Tran_Tran).Where((Tran_Tran x) => x.ReferenceCode == (int?)Code && x.TranTypeID == (int?)TranTypeID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault().ID;
		Tran_Tran tran_Tran = ((IQueryable<Tran_Tran>)db.Tran_Tran).Where((Tran_Tran x) => x.ID == TranID).FirstOrDefault();
		if (tran_Tran != null)
		{
			db.Tran_Tran.Attach(tran_Tran);
			db.Tran_Tran.Remove(tran_Tran);
			result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
		}
		return result;
	}

	public int DeleteTranDetails(int Code, int TranTypeID)
	{
		int num = 0;
		Tran_Tran tran_Tran = ((IQueryable<Tran_Tran>)db.Tran_Tran).Where((Tran_Tran x) => x.ReferenceCode == (int?)Code && x.TranTypeID == (int?)TranTypeID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault();
		int TranID = tran_Tran.ID;
		List<Tran_TranDetails> list = ((IQueryable<Tran_TranDetails>)db.Tran_TranDetails).Where((Tran_TranDetails x) => x.TranSn == (int?)TranID).ToList();
		if (list != null)
		{
			foreach (Tran_TranDetails item in list)
			{
				db.Tran_TranDetails.Attach(item);
				db.Tran_TranDetails.Remove(item);
			}
			num = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
		}
		return TranID;
	}

	public int GetAcctID(int AcctID)
	{
		int result = 0;
		Account_CustSup Row1 = ((IQueryable<Account_CustSup>)db.Account_CustSup).Where((Account_CustSup x) => x.ID == AcctID).FirstOrDefault();
		if (Row1 != null)
		{
			Account_Accounts account_Accounts = ((IQueryable<Account_Accounts>)db.Account_Accounts).Where((Account_Accounts z) => z.Account_No == Row1.AccountNo).FirstOrDefault();
			result = account_Accounts.ID;
		}
		return result;
	}
}

public class Class_PaymentItem
{
	public class MyObject
	{
		public string ItemId { get; set; }

		public int ItemUnitID { get; set; }

		public decimal LastPriceSmall { get; set; }

		public decimal SmallUnitPrice { get; set; }

		public decimal Discount { get; set; }

		public decimal Quantity { get; set; }

		public int StoreID { get; set; }

		public decimal UnitPrice { get; set; }

		public decimal TotalPrice { get; set; }

		public decimal NetUnitPrice { get; set; }

		public decimal NetTotalPrice { get; set; }

		public decimal VAT { get; set; }

		public decimal VAT_discount { get; set; }

		public string UnitType { get; set; }
	}

	private int CachCode = 0;

	}

	public int InsertItems_Complant(DataTable Items, string ItemCode)
	{
		int result = 0;
		foreach (DataRow row in Items.Rows)
		{
			if (row["كود_الصنف"].ToString() != "")
			{
				Item_ItemComponent item_ItemComponent = new Item_ItemComponent();
				string Itemcod = row["كود_الصنف"].ToString();
				string value = Convert.ToString(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == ItemCode).FirstOrDefault().ItemId);
				string value2 = Convert.ToString(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Itemcod).FirstOrDefault().ItemId);
				int ItemRowChek = Convert.ToInt32(value2);
				Item_Items item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items z) => z.ItemId == ItemRowChek).FirstOrDefault();
				if (item_Items != null)
				{
					item_ItemComponent.ItemID_Master = Convert.ToInt32(value);
					item_ItemComponent.ItemID_Complant = Convert.ToInt32(item_Items.ItemId);
					item_ItemComponent.Amount = Convert.ToDecimal(row["الكمية"].ToString());
					string nmae = row["الوحدة"].ToString();
					item_ItemComponent.ItemUnitID = GetUnitIdByName(nmae);
					item_ItemComponent.SmallUnitPrice = Convert.ToDecimal(row["م.التكلفة"].ToString());
					item_ItemComponent.TotalPrice = Math.Round(Convert.ToDecimal(row["الاجمالى"].ToString()), DecimalNum);
					item_ItemComponent.ItemUnitType = row["أسم_الوحدة"].ToString();
					item_ItemComponent.StoreID = Convert.ToInt32(row["رقم المخزن"]);
					db.Item_ItemComponent.Add(item_ItemComponent);
					result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
				}
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	public int ItemQuantityIn(int ItemID, decimal Quantity, string unitType, int StoreID, int itemType)
	{
		GTSdbEntities gTSdbEntities = new GTSdbEntities();
		int result = 0;
		if (ItemID > 0)
		{
			if (itemType == 1)
			{
				ItemQuantity itemQuantity = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)ItemID && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
				Item_Items item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.ItemId == ItemID).FirstOrDefault();
				if (itemQuantity != null)
				{
					ItemQuantity itemQuantity2 = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)ItemID && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
					if (itemQuantity2 != null)
					{
						if (unitType == "الصغرى")
						{
							itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance -= (decimal?)Quantity;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (unitType == "المتوسطة")
						{
							decimal? currentBalance = itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance;
							decimal value = Quantity;
							decimal? convertMediumUnit = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
							itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance = currentBalance - (decimal?)value * convertMediumUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (unitType == "الكبرى")
						{
							decimal? currentBalance2 = itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance;
							decimal value = Quantity;
							decimal? convertMediumUnit2 = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
							itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance = currentBalance2 - (decimal?)value * convertMediumUnit2 * item_Items.ConvertBigUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
					}
					return result;
				}
				ItemQuantity itemQuantity3 = new ItemQuantity();
				int itemQuantityID = Convert.ToInt32(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Select((ItemQuantity p) => p.ItemQuantityID).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Max()) + 1;
				itemQuantity3.ItemQuantityID = itemQuantityID;
				itemQuantity3.ItemID = ItemID;
				if (unitType == "الصغرى")
				{
					itemQuantity3.CurrentBalance = -Quantity;
				}
				if (unitType == "المتوسطة")
				{
					decimal value = -Quantity;
					decimal? currentBalance = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
					itemQuantity3.CurrentBalance = (decimal?)value * currentBalance;
				}
				if (unitType == "الكبرى")
				{
					decimal value = -Quantity;
					decimal? convertMediumUnit3 = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
					itemQuantity3.CurrentBalance = (decimal?)value * convertMediumUnit3 * item_Items.ConvertBigUnit;
				}
				itemQuantity3.BeginningInventory = 0m;
				itemQuantity3.BeginningInventoryPrice = 0m;
				itemQuantity3.OpeningBalance = 0m;
				itemQuantity3.StoreID = StoreID;
				gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities.Add(itemQuantity3);
				result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
			}
			if (itemType == 3)
			{
				List<Item_ItemComponent> list = ((IQueryable<Item_ItemComponent>)gTSdbEntities.Item_ItemComponent).Where((Item_ItemComponent x) => x.ItemID_Master == (int?)ItemID).ToList();
				Item_Items item_Items2 = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.ItemId == ItemID).FirstOrDefault();
				foreach (Item_ItemComponent item in list)
				{
					Item_Items item_Items3 = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => (int?)x.ItemId == item.ItemID_Complant).FirstOrDefault();
					ItemQuantity itemQuantity4 = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == item.ItemID_Complant && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
					if (itemQuantity4 != null)
					{
						ItemQuantity itemQuantity5 = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == item.ItemID_Complant && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
						if (itemQuantity5 == null)
						{
							continue;
						}
						if (unitType == "الصغرى")
						{
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance -= item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance -= item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance -= item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
						}
						if (unitType == "المتوسطة")
						{
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance -= item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance -= item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance -= item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
						}
						if (unitType == "الكبرى")
						{
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance -= item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance -= item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance -= item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
						}
						continue;
					}
					ItemQuantity itemQuantity6 = new ItemQuantity();
					int itemQuantityID2 = Convert.ToInt32(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Select((ItemQuantity p) => p.ItemQuantityID).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Max()) + 1;
					itemQuantity6.ItemQuantityID = itemQuantityID2;
					itemQuantity6.ItemID = item.ItemID_Complant;
					if (unitType == "الصغرى")
					{
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = -item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = -(item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit);
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = -(item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit);
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
					}
					if (unitType == "المتوسطة")
					{
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = -(item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit);
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = -(item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit);
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = -(item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit);
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
					}
					if (unitType == "الكبرى")
					{
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = -(item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit);
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = -(item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit);
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = -(item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit);
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
					}
					itemQuantity6.BeginningInventory = 0m;
					itemQuantity6.BeginningInventoryPrice = 0m;
					itemQuantity6.OpeningBalance = 0m;
					itemQuantity6.StoreID = item.StoreID;
					gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities.Add(itemQuantity6);
					result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
				}
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	public int ItemQuantityOut(int ItemID, decimal Quantity, string unitType, int StoreID, int itemType)
	{
		GTSdbEntities gTSdbEntities = new GTSdbEntities();
		int result = 0;
		if (ItemID > 0)
		{
			if (itemType == 1)
			{
				ItemQuantity itemQuantity = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)ItemID && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
				Item_Items item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.ItemId == ItemID).FirstOrDefault();
				if (itemQuantity != null)
				{
					ItemQuantity itemQuantity2 = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)ItemID && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
					if (itemQuantity2 != null)
					{
						if (unitType == "الصغرى")
						{
							itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance += (decimal?)Quantity;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (unitType == "المتوسطة")
						{
							decimal? currentBalance = itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance;
							decimal value = Quantity;
							decimal? convertMediumUnit = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
							itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance = currentBalance + (decimal?)value * convertMediumUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (unitType == "الكبرى")
						{
							decimal? currentBalance2 = itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance;
							decimal value = Quantity;
							decimal? convertMediumUnit2 = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
							itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance = currentBalance2 + (decimal?)value * convertMediumUnit2 * item_Items.ConvertBigUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
					}
					return result;
				}
				ItemQuantity itemQuantity3 = new ItemQuantity();
				int itemQuantityID = Convert.ToInt32(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Select((ItemQuantity p) => p.ItemQuantityID).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Max()) + 1;
				itemQuantity3.ItemQuantityID = itemQuantityID;
				itemQuantity3.ItemID = ItemID;
				if (unitType == "الصغرى")
				{
					itemQuantity3.CurrentBalance = Quantity;
				}
				if (unitType == "المتوسطة")
				{
					decimal value = Quantity;
					decimal? currentBalance = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
					itemQuantity3.CurrentBalance = (decimal?)value * currentBalance;
				}
				if (unitType == "الكبرى")
				{
					decimal value = Quantity;
					decimal? convertMediumUnit3 = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
					itemQuantity3.CurrentBalance = (decimal?)value * convertMediumUnit3 * item_Items.ConvertBigUnit;
				}
				itemQuantity3.BeginningInventory = 0m;
				itemQuantity3.BeginningInventoryPrice = 0m;
				itemQuantity3.OpeningBalance = 0m;
				itemQuantity3.StoreID = StoreID;
				gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities.Add(itemQuantity3);
				result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
			}
			if (itemType == 3)
			{
				List<Item_ItemComponent> list = ((IQueryable<Item_ItemComponent>)gTSdbEntities.Item_ItemComponent).Where((Item_ItemComponent x) => x.ItemID_Master == (int?)ItemID).ToList();
				Item_Items item_Items2 = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.ItemId == ItemID).FirstOrDefault();
				foreach (Item_ItemComponent item in list)
				{
					Item_Items item_Items3 = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => (int?)x.ItemId == item.ItemID_Complant).FirstOrDefault();
					ItemQuantity itemQuantity4 = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == item.ItemID_Complant && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
					if (itemQuantity4 != null)
					{
						ItemQuantity itemQuantity5 = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == item.ItemID_Complant && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
						if (itemQuantity5 == null)
						{
							continue;
						}
						if (unitType == "الصغرى")
						{
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance += item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance += item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance += item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
						}
						if (unitType == "المتوسطة")
						{
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance += item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance += item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance += item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
						}
						if (unitType == "الكبرى")
						{
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance += item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance += item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
							if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
							{
								itemQuantity5.CurrentBalance += item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit;
								result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
							}
						}
						continue;
					}
					ItemQuantity itemQuantity6 = new ItemQuantity();
					int itemQuantityID2 = Convert.ToInt32(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Select((ItemQuantity p) => p.ItemQuantityID).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Max()) + 1;
					itemQuantity6.ItemQuantityID = itemQuantityID2;
					itemQuantity6.ItemID = item.ItemID_Complant;
					if (unitType == "الصغرى")
					{
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
					}
					if (unitType == "المتوسطة")
					{
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit * item_Items2.ConvertMediumUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
					}
					if (unitType == "الكبرى")
					{
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
						if (item.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى")
						{
							itemQuantity6.CurrentBalance = item.Amount * (decimal?)Quantity * item_Items3.ConvertMediumUnit * item_Items3.ConvertBigUnit * item_Items2.ConvertBigUnit;
							result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
						}
					}
					itemQuantity6.BeginningInventory = 0m;
					itemQuantity6.BeginningInventoryPrice = 0m;
					itemQuantity6.OpeningBalance = 0m;
					itemQuantity6.StoreID = StoreID;
					gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities.Add(itemQuantity6);
					result = ((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
				}
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	public string GetStoreName()
	{
		int StoreID = Convert.ToInt32((from x in (IQueryable<TblSetting>)db.TblSettings
			where x.ID == LoginDetails.BranchID
			select x.StoreID).SingleOrDefault());
		return (from x in (IQueryable<Account_Stores>)db.Account_Stores
			where x.ID == StoreID
			select x.Store_Name).FirstOrDefault();
	}

	public decimal GetQantityForItems(string itemCode, string unit)
	{
		decimal num = 0m;
		int Itemid = (from x in GetItems(itemCode)
			select x.ItemId).FirstOrDefault();
		decimal num2 = Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)Itemid).Sum((ItemQuantity x) => x.OpeningBalance));
		decimal num3 = Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)Itemid).Sum((ItemQuantity x) => x.CurrentBalance));
		num = num2 + num3;
		if (unit == "المتوسطة")
		{
			num /= Convert.ToDecimal((from x in GetItems(itemCode)
				select x.ConvertMediumUnit).FirstOrDefault());
		}
		if (unit == "الكبرى")
		{
			num /= Convert.ToDecimal((from x in GetItems(itemCode)
				select x.ConvertMediumUnit).FirstOrDefault());
			num /= Convert.ToDecimal((from x in GetItems(itemCode)
				select x.ConvertBigUnit).FirstOrDefault());
		}
		return num;
	}

	public decimal GetQantityForItems(string itemCode, string unit, int storeid)
	{
		decimal num = 0m;
		int Itemid = (from x in GetItems(itemCode)
			select x.ItemId).FirstOrDefault();
		decimal num2 = Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)Itemid && x.StoreID == (int?)storeid).Sum((ItemQuantity x) => x.OpeningBalance));
		decimal num3 = Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)Itemid && x.StoreID == (int?)storeid).Sum((ItemQuantity x) => x.CurrentBalance));
		num = num2 + num3;
		if (unit == "المتوسطة")
		{
			num /= Convert.ToDecimal((from x in GetItems(itemCode)
				select x.ConvertMediumUnit).FirstOrDefault());
		}
		if (unit == "الكبرى")
		{
			num /= Convert.ToDecimal((from x in GetItems(itemCode)
				select x.ConvertMediumUnit).FirstOrDefault());
			num /= Convert.ToDecimal((from x in GetItems(itemCode)
				select x.ConvertBigUnit).FirstOrDefault());
		}
		return num;
	}

	public MyObject ConvertRowToMyObject(DataRow row)
	{
		MyObject myObject = new MyObject();
		if (row["كود_الصنف"].ToString() != "")
		{
			string Itemcod = row["كود_الصنف"].ToString();
			myObject.ItemId = Convert.ToString(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Itemcod).FirstOrDefault().ItemId);
			myObject.SmallUnitPrice = Convert.ToDecimal(row["سعر الوحدة"].ToString());
			myObject.UnitPrice = Convert.ToDecimal(row["سعر الوحدة"].ToString());
			myObject.TotalPrice = Convert.ToDecimal(row["الاجمالى"].ToString());
			myObject.Quantity = Convert.ToDecimal(row["الكمية"].ToString());
			myObject.NetUnitPrice = Convert.ToDecimal(row["سعربعدالخصم"].ToString());
			myObject.NetTotalPrice = Convert.ToDecimal(row["إجمالى_بعدالخصم"].ToString());
			myObject.VAT = Convert.ToDecimal(row["الضربية"].ToString());
			myObject.VAT_discount = Convert.ToDecimal(row["الضريبه_بعد_الخصم"].ToString());
			myObject.UnitType = row["أسم_الوحدة"].ToString();
			string nmae = row["الوحدة"].ToString();
			myObject.ItemUnitID = GetUnitIdByName(nmae);
		}
		return myObject;
	}

	public int GetUnitIdByName(string Nmae)

		int num = 0;
		return (from x in (IQueryable<Item_Unit>)db.Item_Unit
			where x.Name == Nmae
			select x.ID).FirstOrDefault();
	}

	public int InsertItems(DataTable Items, int Purchese_ID)
	{
		int result = 0;
		foreach (DataRow row in Items.Rows)
		{
			if (!(row["كود_الصنف"].ToString() != ""))
			{
				continue;
			}
			Order_PaymentItemDetails order_PaymentItemDetails = new Order_PaymentItemDetails();
			string Itemcod = row["كود_الصنف"].ToString();
			string value = Convert.ToString(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Itemcod).FirstOrDefault().ItemId);
			int ItemRowChek = Convert.ToInt32(value);
			Item_Items item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items z) => z.ItemId == ItemRowChek).FirstOrDefault();
			int value2 = Convert.ToInt32(((IQueryable<TblSetting>)db.TblSettings).Select((TblSetting x) => x.StoreID).FirstOrDefault());
			if (item_Items == null)
			{
				continue;
			}
			order_PaymentItemDetails.Purchese_ID = Purchese_ID;
			order_PaymentItemDetails.BranchID = LoginDetails.BranchID;
			order_PaymentItemDetails.ItemID = Convert.ToInt32(item_Items.ItemId);
			string nmae = row["الوحدة"].ToString();
			order_PaymentItemDetails.ItemUnitID = GetUnitIdByName(nmae);
			order_PaymentItemDetails.SmallUnitPrice = Convert.ToDecimal(row["سعر الوحدة"].ToString());
			order_PaymentItemDetails.Quantity = Convert.ToDecimal(row["الكمية"].ToString());
			order_PaymentItemDetails.StoreID = value2;
			order_PaymentItemDetails.LastCost = Math.Round(Convert.ToDecimal(row["متوسط_التكلفة"].ToString()), DecimalNum);
			order_PaymentItemDetails.UnitPrice = Math.Round(Convert.ToDecimal(row["سعر الوحدة"].ToString()), DecimalNum);
			order_PaymentItemDetails.TotalPrice = Math.Round(Convert.ToDecimal(row["الاجمالى"].ToString()), DecimalNum);
			order_PaymentItemDetails.NetUnitPrice = Math.Round(Convert.ToDecimal(row["سعربعدالخصم"].ToString()), DecimalNum);
			order_PaymentItemDetails.NetTotalPrice = Math.Round(Convert.ToDecimal(row["إجمالى_بعدالخصم"].ToString()), DecimalNum);
			order_PaymentItemDetails.VAT = Math.Round(Convert.ToDecimal(row["الضربية"].ToString()), DecimalNum);
			order_PaymentItemDetails.ItemUnitType = row["أسم_الوحدة"].ToString();
			order_PaymentItemDetails.VAT_Discount = Math.Round(Convert.ToDecimal(row["الضريبه_بعد_الخصم"].ToString()), DecimalNum);
			order_PaymentItemDetails.StoreID = Convert.ToInt32(row["رقم المخزن"]);
			db.Order_PaymentItemDetails.Add(order_PaymentItemDetails);
			result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
			if (item_Items.item_Type == 1)
			{
				ItemQuantityIn(Convert.ToInt32(value), Convert.ToDecimal(row["الكمية"].ToString()), row["أسم_الوحدة"].ToString(), Convert.ToInt32(row["رقم المخزن"].ToString()), 1);
			}
			if (item_Items.item_Type != 3)
			{
				continue;
			}
			decimal num = SLCTQuantity(Convert.ToInt32(value), Convert.ToInt32(row["رقم المخزن"].ToString()));
			decimal num2 = Convert.ToDecimal(row["الكمية"].ToString());
			if (num > 0m)
			{
				if (num - num2 >= 0m)
				{
					ItemQuantityIn(Convert.ToInt32(value), num2, row["أسم_الوحدة"].ToString(), Convert.ToInt32(row["رقم المخزن"].ToString()), 1);
					continue;
				}
				ItemQuantityIn(Convert.ToInt32(value), num, row["أسم_الوحدة"].ToString(), Convert.ToInt32(row["رقم المخزن"].ToString()), 1);
				ItemQuantityIn(Convert.ToInt32(value), num2 - num, row["أسم_الوحدة"].ToString(), Convert.ToInt32(row["رقم المخزن"].ToString()), DecimalNum);
			}
			else
			{
				ItemQuantityIn(Convert.ToInt32(value), num2, row["أسم_الوحدة"].ToString(), Convert.ToInt32(row["رقم المخزن"].ToString()), DecimalNum);
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	public decimal SLCTQuantity(int ItmID, int StoreID)
	{
		GTSdbEntities gTSdbEntities = new GTSdbEntities();
		int num = 0;
		ItemQuantity itemQuantity = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)gTSdbEntities.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)ItmID && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
		num = ((itemQuantity != null) ? Convert.ToInt32(itemQuantity.CurrentBalance) : 0);
		return num;
	}

	public int InsertPurch(out int Code, int SupplierID, string SupplierName, DateTime Purchases_Date, int CostCenter, int ProjectId, string Note, string NoteNum, decimal CostOrder, decimal TotalPrices, decimal Tax, decimal Safy, decimal DiscountNum, decimal DiscountPerantage, decimal Tax_Discount, decimal TotalPrices_Discount, decimal Net, decimal CashMoney, decimal CashBank, bool cashierType, decimal TobaccoTax, decimal AllTax)
	{
		int num = 0;
		Code = 0;
		int iD = Convert.ToInt32(((IQueryable<Order_PaymentItem>)db.Order_PaymentItem).Select((Order_PaymentItem p) => p.ID).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Max()) + 1;
		int num2 = Convert.ToInt32((from p in (IQueryable<Order_PaymentItem>)db.Order_PaymentItem
			where p.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID
			select p.PurBranchID).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Max()) + 1;
		Order_PaymentItem order_PaymentItem = new Order_PaymentItem();
		order_PaymentItem.ID = iD;
		CachCode = num2;
		Code = num2;
		order_PaymentItem.BranchID = LoginDetails.BranchID;
		order_PaymentItem.PurBranchID = num2;
		order_PaymentItem.SupplierID = SupplierID;
		order_PaymentItem.SupplierName = SupplierName;
		order_PaymentItem.Purchases_Date = Purchases_Date;
		order_PaymentItem.CostCentersID = CostCenter;
		order_PaymentItem.ProjectId = ProjectId;
		order_PaymentItem.Note = Note;
		order_PaymentItem.NoteNum = NoteNum;
		order_PaymentItem.CostOrder = CostOrder;
		order_PaymentItem.TotalPrices = TotalPrices;
		order_PaymentItem.Tax = Tax;
		order_PaymentItem.Safy = Safy;
		order_PaymentItem.DiscountNum = DiscountNum;
		order_PaymentItem.DiscountPerantage = DiscountPerantage;
		order_PaymentItem.Tax_Discount = Tax_Discount;
		order_PaymentItem.TotalPrices_Discount = TotalPrices_Discount;
		order_PaymentItem.Net = Net;
		order_PaymentItem.OrderCashierType = cashierType;
		order_PaymentItem.CashMoney = CashMoney;
		order_PaymentItem.CashBank = CashBank;
		order_PaymentItem.TobaccoTax = TobaccoTax;
		order_PaymentItem.AllTax = AllTax;
		order_PaymentItem.UserID_Add = LoginDetails.UserID;
		order_PaymentItem.UserBranch_Add = LoginDetails.BranchID;
		order_PaymentItem.UserMacAddress_Add = LoginDetails.macAddress;
		order_PaymentItem.UserDate_Add = DateTime.Now;
		db.Order_PaymentItem.Add(order_PaymentItem);
		num = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
		return num2;
	}

	public void deleteItemsByPurcahesId(int PurID)
	{
		List<Order_PaymentItemDetails> list = ((IQueryable<Order_PaymentItemDetails>)db.Order_PaymentItemDetails).Where((Order_PaymentItemDetails x) => x.Purchese_ID == (int?)PurID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).ToList();
		if (list == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		foreach (Order_PaymentItemDetails item in list)
		{
			Item_Items item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => (int?)x.ItemId == item.ItemID).FirstOrDefault();
			ItemQuantityOut(Convert.ToInt32(item.ItemID), Convert.ToDecimal(item.Quantity), item.ItemUnitType, Convert.ToInt32(item.StoreID), Convert.ToInt32(item_Items.item_Type));
			db.Order_PaymentItemDetails.Attach(item);
			db.Order_PaymentItemDetails.Remove(item);
		}
		((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
	}

	public int UpdatePurch(int PurID, int SupplierID, string SupplierName, DateTime Purchases_Date, int CostCenter, int ProjectId, string Note, string NoteNum, decimal CostOrder, decimal TotalPrices, decimal Tax, decimal Safy, decimal DiscountNum, decimal DiscountPerantage, decimal Tax_Discount, decimal TotalPrices_Discount, decimal Net, decimal CashMoney, decimal CashBank, bool cashierType, decimal TobaccoTax, decimal AllTax)
	{
		int result = 0;
		Order_PaymentItem order_PaymentItem = ((IQueryable<Order_PaymentItem>)db.Order_PaymentItem).Where((Order_PaymentItem x) => x.PurBranchID == (int?)PurID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault();
		int iD = ((IQueryable<Order_PaymentItem>)db.Order_PaymentItem).Where((Order_PaymentItem x) => x.PurBranchID == (int?)PurID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault().ID;
		if (order_PaymentItem != null)
		{
			order_PaymentItem.SupplierID = SupplierID;
			order_PaymentItem.SupplierName = SupplierName;
			order_PaymentItem.Purchases_Date = Purchases_Date;
			order_PaymentItem.CostCentersID = CostCenter;
			order_PaymentItem.ProjectId = ProjectId;
			order_PaymentItem.Note = Note;
			order_PaymentItem.NoteNum = NoteNum;
			order_PaymentItem.TotalPrices = TotalPrices;
			order_PaymentItem.Tax = Tax;
			order_PaymentItem.Safy = Safy;
			order_PaymentItem.CostOrder = CostOrder;
			order_PaymentItem.DiscountNum = DiscountNum;
			order_PaymentItem.DiscountPerantage = DiscountPerantage;
			order_PaymentItem.Tax_Discount = Tax_Discount;
			order_PaymentItem.TotalPrices_Discount = TotalPrices_Discount;
			order_PaymentItem.Net = Net;
			order_PaymentItem.CashMoney = CashMoney;
			order_PaymentItem.CashBank = CashBank;
			order_PaymentItem.OrderCashierType = cashierType;
			order_PaymentItem.TobaccoTax = TobaccoTax;
			order_PaymentItem.AllTax = AllTax;
			order_PaymentItem.UserID_Update = LoginDetails.UserID;
			order_PaymentItem.UserBranch_Update = LoginDetails.GroupID;
			order_PaymentItem.UserMacAddress_Update = LoginDetails.macAddress;
			order_PaymentItem.UserDate_Update = DateTime.Now;
			((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
			result = PurID;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public int Delete(int PurID)
	{
		int result = 0;
		Order_PaymentItem order_PaymentItem = ((IQueryable<Order_PaymentItem>)db.Order_PaymentItem).Where((Order_PaymentItem x) => x.PurBranchID == (int?)PurID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault();
		if (order_PaymentItem != null)
		{
			deleteItemsByPurcahesId(PurID);
			db.Order_PaymentItem.Attach(order_PaymentItem);
			db.Order_PaymentItem.Remove(order_PaymentItem);
			result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
		}
		return result;
	}

	public int DeleteTran(int Code)
	{
		int result = 0;
		try
		{
			int TranID = ((IQueryable<Tran_Tran>)db.Tran_Tran).Where((Tran_Tran x) => x.ReferenceCode == (int?)Code && x.TranTypeID == (int?)19 && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault().ID;
			Tran_Tran tran_Tran = ((IQueryable<Tran_Tran>)db.Tran_Tran).Where((Tran_Tran x) => x.ID == TranID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault();
			if (tran_Tran != null)
			{
				db.Tran_Tran.Attach(tran_Tran);
				db.Tran_Tran.Remove(tran_Tran);
				result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
		return result;
	}

	public int DeleteTranDetails(int Code)
	{
		int result = 0;
		try
		{
			int TranID = ((IQueryable<Tran_Tran>)db.Tran_Tran).Where((Tran_Tran x) => x.ReferenceCode == (int?)Code && x.TranTypeID == (int?)19 && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).FirstOrDefault().ID;
			List<Tran_TranDetails> list = ((IQueryable<Tran_TranDetails>)db.Tran_TranDetails).Where((Tran_TranDetails x) => x.TranSn == (int?)TranID && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).ToList();
			if (list != null)
			{
				foreach (Tran_TranDetails item in list)
				{
					db.Tran_TranDetails.Attach(item);
					db.Tran_TranDetails.Remove(item);
				}
				result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
		return result;
	}

	public decimal GetSellPermission(string Item_code, int UserSellPricePer, string Type)
	{
		decimal result = 0m;
		try
		{
			switch (Type)
			{
			case "الصغرى":
				if (UserSellPricePer == 1)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().SellPriceSmall);
				}
				if (UserSellPricePer == 2)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().SellPriceSmall2);
				}
				if (UserSellPricePer == 3)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().SellPriceSmall3);
				}
				if (UserSellPricePer >= 4)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().LastCost);
				}
				break;
			case "المتوسطة":
				if (UserSellPricePer == 1)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().SellPriceMedium);
				}
				if (UserSellPricePer == 2)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().SellPriceMedium2);
				}
				if (UserSellPricePer == 3)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().SellPriceMedium3);
				}
				if (UserSellPricePer >= 4)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().LastCost2);
				}
				break;
			case "الكبرى":
				if (UserSellPricePer == 1)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().SellpriceLarge);
				}
				if (UserSellPricePer == 2)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().SellpriceLarge2);
				}
				if (UserSellPricePer == 3)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().SellpriceLarge3);
				}
				if (UserSellPricePer >= 4)
				{
					return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Item_code).FirstOrDefault().LastCost3);
				}
				break;
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			result = 0m;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public decimal GetQuantityPermission(int ItemID, int StoreID, string Type)
	{
		decimal result = 0m;
		try
		{
			switch (Type)
			{
			case "الصغرى":
				return Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)ItemID && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault().CurrentBalance);
			case "المتوسطة":
			{
				decimal num4 = Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.ItemId == ItemID).FirstOrDefault().ConvertMediumUnit);
				decimal num5 = Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)ItemID && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault().CurrentBalance);
				return num5 / num4;
			}
			case "الكبرى":
			{
				Item_Items item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.ItemId == ItemID).FirstOrDefault();
				decimal num = Convert.ToDecimal(item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit);
				decimal num2 = Convert.ToDecimal(item_Items.ConvertBigUnit);
				decimal num3 = Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)ItemID && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault().CurrentBalance);
				return num3 / (num * num2);
			}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			result = 0m;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public int GetAccID(int Acc)
	{
		int num = 0;
		return ((IQueryable<Account_Accounts>)db.Account_Accounts).Where((Account_Accounts x) => x.Account_No == (int?)Acc).FirstOrDefault().ID;
	}
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Data;
using System.Data.Entity;
using System.Data.SqlClient;
using System.Linq;

namespace GTSErpSystem.BLL.Orders;

internal class Class_Purchases
{
	private int CachCode = 0;

	private DataTable dtCurrentTable = new DataTable();

	private GTSdbEntities db = new GTSdbEntities();

	private SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection("Data Source=" + LoginDetails.nameServer + ";Initial Catalog=" + LoginDetails.databaseServer + ";Persist Security Info=True;User ID=" + LoginDetails.userNameServer + ";Password=" + LoginDetails.passServer);

	private int DecimalNum = LoginDetails.DecimalNum;

	public List<Account_AccountType> GetAccountType()
	{
		return ((IEnumerable<Account_AccountType>)db.Account_AccountType).ToList();
	}

	public List<Select_SearchAccountCostCenter_Result> GetCostCenters()
	{
		return ((IEnumerable<Select_SearchAccountCostCenter_Result>)db.Select_SearchAccountCostCenter(Convert.ToInt32(LoginDetails.BranchID))).ToList();
	}

	public List<Order_Purchases> GetPurchases()

	}

	public List<Order_Purchases> GetPurchases(int Code)
	{
		return ((IQueryable<Order_Purchases>)db.Order_Purchases).Where((Order_Purchases x) => x.PurBranchID == (int?)Code && x.BranchID == (int?)LoginDetails.BranchID).ToList();
	}

	public int InsertItems_Complant(DataTable Items, string ItemCode)
	{
		int result = 0;
		foreach (DataRow row in Items.Rows)
		{
			if (row["كود_الصنف"].ToString() != "")
			{
				Item_ItemComponent item_ItemComponent = new Item_ItemComponent();
				string Itemcod = row["كود_الصنف"].ToString();
				string value = Convert.ToString(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == ItemCode).FirstOrDefault().ItemId);
				string value2 = Convert.ToString(((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Itemcod).FirstOrDefault().ItemId);
				int ItemRowChek = Convert.ToInt32(value2);
				Item_Items item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items z) => z.ItemId == ItemRowChek).FirstOrDefault();
				if (item_Items != null)
				{
					item_ItemComponent.ItemID_Master = Convert.ToInt32(value);
					item_ItemComponent.ItemID_Complant = Convert.ToInt32(item_Items.ItemId);
					item_ItemComponent.Amount = Convert.ToDecimal(row["الكمية"].ToString());
					string nmae = row["الوحدة"].ToString();
					item_ItemComponent.ItemUnitID = GetUnitIdByName(nmae);
					item_ItemComponent.SmallUnitPrice = Convert.ToDecimal(row["م.التكلفة"].ToString());
					item_ItemComponent.TotalPrice = Math.Round(Convert.ToDecimal(row["الاجمالى"].ToString()), DecimalNum);
					item_ItemComponent.ItemUnitType = row["أسم_الوحدة"].ToString();
					item_ItemComponent.StoreID = Convert.ToInt32(row["رقم المخزن"]);
					db.Item_ItemComponent.Add(item_ItemComponent);
					result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
				}
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	public int ItemQuantityIn(int ItemID, decimal Quantity, string unitType, int StoreID, DateTime ExpireDate, decimal UnitNumber)
	{
		int result = 0;
		if (ItemID > 0)
		{
			DateTime dateTime = Convert.ToDateTime(ExpireDate.ToString("d"));
			ItemQuantity itemQuantity = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)ItemID && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
			if (LoginDetails.IsPharmacy)
			{
				itemQuantity = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)ItemID && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
			}
			Item_Items item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.ItemId == ItemID).FirstOrDefault();
			if (itemQuantity != null)
			{
				ItemQuantity itemQuantity2 = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)ItemID && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
				if (itemQuantity2 != null)
				{
					if (unitType == "الصغرى")
					{
						itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance += (decimal?)Quantity;
						itemQuantity2.UnitNumber += (decimal?)UnitNumber;
						result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
					}
					if (unitType == "المتوسطة")
					{
						decimal? currentBalance = itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance;
						decimal value = Quantity;
						decimal? convertMediumUnit = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
						itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance = currentBalance + (decimal?)value * convertMediumUnit;
						itemQuantity2.UnitNumber += (decimal?)UnitNumber;
						result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
					}
					if (unitType == "الكبرى")
					{
						decimal? currentBalance2 = itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance;
						decimal value = Quantity;
						decimal? convertMediumUnit2 = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
						itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance = currentBalance2 + (decimal?)value * convertMediumUnit2 * item_Items.ConvertBigUnit;
						itemQuantity2.UnitNumber += (decimal?)UnitNumber;
						result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
					}
				}
				return result;
			}
			ItemQuantity itemQuantity3 = new ItemQuantity();
			int itemQuantityID = Convert.ToInt32(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Select((ItemQuantity p) => p.ItemQuantityID).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Max()) + 1;
			itemQuantity3.ItemQuantityID = itemQuantityID;
			itemQuantity3.ItemID = ItemID;
			if (unitType == "الصغرى")
			{
				itemQuantity3.CurrentBalance = Quantity;
			}
			if (unitType == "المتوسطة")
			{
				decimal value = Quantity;
				decimal? currentBalance = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
				itemQuantity3.CurrentBalance = (decimal?)value * currentBalance;
			}
			if (unitType == "الكبرى")
			{
				decimal value = Quantity;
				decimal? convertMediumUnit3 = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
				itemQuantity3.CurrentBalance = (decimal?)value * convertMediumUnit3 * item_Items.ConvertBigUnit;
			}
			itemQuantity3.UnitNumber = UnitNumber;
			itemQuantity3.BeginningInventory = 0m;
			itemQuantity3.BeginningInventoryPrice = 0m;
			itemQuantity3.OpeningBalance = 0m;
			itemQuantity3.StoreID = StoreID;
			db.ItemQuantities.Add(itemQuantity3);
			result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
		}
		return result;
	}

	public string GetStoreName()

		int StoreID = Convert.ToInt32((from x in (IQueryable<TblSetting>)db.TblSettings
			where x.ID == LoginDetails.BranchID

			where x.ID == StoreID
			select x.Store_Name).FirstOrDefault();
	}

			where x.ID == LoginDetails.BranchID
			select x.StoreID).SingleOrDefault());
	}

			where x.ID == LoginDetails.BranchID
			select x.IsVat).FirstOrDefault());
	}

	public int ItemQuantityOut(int ItemID, decimal Quantity, string unitType, int StoreID, decimal UnitNumber)
	{
		int result = 0;
		if (ItemID > 0)
		{
			ItemQuantity itemQuantity = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)ItemID && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
			if (LoginDetails.IsPharmacy)
			{
				itemQuantity = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)ItemID && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
			}
			Item_Items item_Items = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.ItemId == ItemID).FirstOrDefault();
			if (itemQuantity != null)
			{
				ItemQuantity itemQuantity2 = ((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)ItemID && x.StoreID == (int?)StoreID).FirstOrDefault();
				if (unitType == "الصغرى")
				{
					itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance -= (decimal?)Quantity;
					itemQuantity2.UnitNumber -= (decimal?)UnitNumber;
					result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
				}
				if (unitType == "المتوسطة")
				{
					decimal? currentBalance = itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance;
					decimal value = Quantity;
					decimal? convertMediumUnit = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
					itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance = currentBalance - (decimal?)value * convertMediumUnit;
					itemQuantity2.UnitNumber -= (decimal?)UnitNumber;
					result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
				}
				if (unitType == "الكبرى")
				{
					decimal? currentBalance2 = itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance;
					decimal value = Quantity;
					decimal? convertBigUnit = item_Items.ConvertBigUnit;
					itemQuantity2.CurrentBalance = currentBalance2 - (decimal?)value * convertBigUnit * item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
					itemQuantity2.UnitNumber -= (decimal?)UnitNumber;
					result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
				}
				return result;
			}
			ItemQuantity itemQuantity3 = new ItemQuantity();
			int itemQuantityID = Convert.ToInt32(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Select((ItemQuantity p) => p.ItemQuantityID).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Max()) + 1;
			itemQuantity3.ItemQuantityID = itemQuantityID;
			itemQuantity3.ItemID = ItemID;
			if (unitType == "الصغرى")
			{
				itemQuantity3.CurrentBalance = -Quantity;
			}
			if (unitType == "المتوسطة")
			{
				decimal value = Quantity;
				decimal? currentBalance = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
				itemQuantity3.CurrentBalance = -((decimal?)value * currentBalance);
			}
			if (unitType == "الكبرى")
			{
				decimal value = Quantity;
				decimal? convertMediumUnit2 = item_Items.ConvertMediumUnit;
				itemQuantity3.CurrentBalance = -((decimal?)value * convertMediumUnit2 * item_Items.ConvertBigUnit);
			}
			itemQuantity3.UnitNumber = -UnitNumber;
			itemQuantity3.BeginningInventory = 0m;
			itemQuantity3.BeginningInventoryPrice = 0m;
			itemQuantity3.OpeningBalance = 0m;
			itemQuantity3.StoreID = StoreID;
			db.ItemQuantities.Add(itemQuantity3);
			result = ((DbContext)db).SaveChanges();
		}
		return result;
	}

	public List<Item_Items> GetItems(string Code)
	{
		GTSdbEntities gTSdbEntities = new GTSdbEntities();
		List<Order_PurchasesDetails> list = new List<Order_PurchasesDetails>();
		gTSdbEntities.Order_PurchasesDetails.AddRange((IEnumerable<Order_PurchasesDetails>)list);
		return ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items x) => x.Item_code == Code || x.item_Name == Code || x.item_Name_English == Code || x.SmallBarCode1 == Code || x.SmallBarCode2 == Code || x.SmallBarCode3 == Code || x.MediumBarCode1 == Code || x.MediumBarCode2 == Code || x.MediumBarCode3 == Code || x.BigBarCode1 == Code || x.BigBarCode2 == Code || x.BigBarCode3 == Code).ToList();
	}

	public decimal GetQantityForItems(string itemCode, string unit)
	{
		decimal num = 0m;
		int Itemid = (from x in GetItems(itemCode)
			select x.ItemId).FirstOrDefault();
		decimal num2 = Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)Itemid).Sum((ItemQuantity x) => x.OpeningBalance));
		decimal num3 = Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)Itemid).Sum((ItemQuantity x) => x.CurrentBalance));
		num = num2 + num3;
		if (unit == "المتوسطة")
		{
			num /= Convert.ToDecimal((from x in GetItems(itemCode)
				select x.ConvertMediumUnit).FirstOrDefault());
		}
		if (unit == "الكبرى")
		{
			num /= Convert.ToDecimal((from x in GetItems(itemCode)
				select x.ConvertMediumUnit).FirstOrDefault());
			num /= Convert.ToDecimal((from x in GetItems(itemCode)
				select x.ConvertBigUnit).FirstOrDefault());
		}
		return num;
	}

	public decimal GetQantityForItems(string itemCode, string unit, int storeid)
	{
		decimal num = 0m;
		int Itemid = (from x in GetItems(itemCode)
			select x.ItemId).FirstOrDefault();
		decimal num2 = Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)Itemid && x.StoreID == (int?)storeid).Sum((ItemQuantity x) => x.OpeningBalance));
		decimal num3 = Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<ItemQuantity>)db.ItemQuantities).Where((ItemQuantity x) => x.ItemID == (int?)Itemid && x.StoreID == (int?)storeid).Sum((ItemQuantity x) => x.CurrentBalance));
		num = num2 + num3;
		if (unit == "المتوسطة")
		{
			num /= Convert.ToDecimal((from x in GetItems(itemCode)
				select x.ConvertMediumUnit).FirstOrDefault());
		}
		if (unit == "الكبرى")
		{
			num /= Convert.ToDecimal((from x in GetItems(itemCode)
				select x.ConvertMediumUnit).FirstOrDefault());
			num /= Convert.ToDecimal((from x in GetItems(itemCode)
				select x.ConvertBigUnit).FirstOrDefault());
		}
		return num;
	}

	public void Get_Item_AvargeCost(string itemcode, string unittype, decimal Lastcost)
	{
		try
		{
			GTSdbEntities gTSdbEntities = new GTSdbEntities();
			try
			{
				Item_Items ItemDtails = ((IQueryable<Item_Items>)gTSdbEntities.Item_Items).Where((Item_Items y) => y.Item_code == itemcode).FirstOrDefault();
				if (ItemDtails == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				decimal num = 0m;
				decimal num2 = 0m;
				decimal num3 = 0m;
				decimal num4 = Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Order_PurchasesDetails>)gTSdbEntities.Order_PurchasesDetails).Where((Order_PurchasesDetails x) => x.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى" && x.ItemID == (int?)ItemDtails.ItemId).Sum((Order_PurchasesDetails x) => x.Quantity));
				decimal num5 = Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Order_PurchasesDetails>)gTSdbEntities.Order_PurchasesDetails).Where((Order_PurchasesDetails x) => x.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة" && x.ItemID == (int?)ItemDtails.ItemId).Sum((Order_PurchasesDetails x) => x.Quantity));
				decimal num6 = Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Order_PurchasesDetails>)gTSdbEntities.Order_PurchasesDetails).Where((Order_PurchasesDetails x) => x.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى" && x.ItemID == (int?)ItemDtails.ItemId).Sum((Order_PurchasesDetails x) => x.Quantity));
				decimal num7 = Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Order_PurchasesDetails>)gTSdbEntities.Order_PurchasesDetails).Where((Order_PurchasesDetails x) => x.ItemUnitType == "الصغرى" && x.ItemID == (int?)ItemDtails.ItemId).Sum((Order_PurchasesDetails x) => x.NetTotalPrice));
				decimal num8 = Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Order_PurchasesDetails>)gTSdbEntities.Order_PurchasesDetails).Where((Order_PurchasesDetails x) => x.ItemUnitType == "المتوسطة" && x.ItemID == (int?)ItemDtails.ItemId).Sum((Order_PurchasesDetails x) => x.NetTotalPrice));
				decimal num9 = Convert.ToDecimal(((IQueryable<Order_PurchasesDetails>)gTSdbEntities.Order_PurchasesDetails).Where((Order_PurchasesDetails x) => x.ItemUnitType == "الكبرى" && x.ItemID == (int?)ItemDtails.ItemId).Sum((Order_PurchasesDetails x) => x.NetTotalPrice));
				decimal value = num4;
				decimal value2 = num5;
				decimal? convertMediumUnit = ItemDtails.ConvertMediumUnit;
				decimal? num10 = (decimal?)value2 * convertMediumUnit;
				decimal? num11 = (decimal?)value + num10;
				value = num6;
				decimal? convertBigUnit = ItemDtails.ConvertBigUnit;
				num3 = Convert.ToDecimal(num11 + (decimal?)value * convertBigUnit * ItemDtails.ConvertMediumUnit);
				num2 = Convert.ToDecimal(num7 + num8 + num9);
				num = ((!(num3 == 0m)) ? (num2 / num3) : Lastcost);
				ItemDtails.Average_cost = num;
				if (unittype == "الصغرى")
				{
					ItemDtails.LastCost = Lastcost;
					if (ItemDtails.UnitMedium > 0)
					{
						Item_Items item_Items = ItemDtails;
						value = Lastcost;
						decimal? convertMediumUnit2 = ItemDtails.ConvertMediumUnit;
						item_Items.LastCost2 = (decimal?)value * convertMediumUnit2;
					}
					if (ItemDtails.UnitLarge > 0)
					{
						Item_Items item_Items2 = ItemDtails;
						value = Lastcost;
						convertMediumUnit = ItemDtails.ConvertMediumUnit;
						item_Items2.LastCost3 = (decimal?)value * convertMediumUnit * ItemDtails.ConvertBigUnit;
					}
				}
				if (unittype == "المتوسطة")
				{
					ItemDtails.LastCost2 = Lastcost;
					Item_Items item_Items3 = ItemDtails;
					value = Lastcost;
					num11 = ItemDtails.ConvertMediumUnit;
					item_Items3.LastCost = (decimal?)value / num11;
					if (ItemDtails.UnitLarge > 0)
					{
						Item_Items item_Items4 = ItemDtails;
						value = Lastcost;
						num11 = ItemDtails.ConvertBigUnit;
						item_Items4.LastCost3 = (decimal?)value * num11;
					}
				}
				if (unittype == "الكبرى")
				{
					ItemDtails.LastCost3 = Lastcost;
					Item_Items item_Items5 = ItemDtails;
					value = Lastcost;
					num11 = ItemDtails.ConvertBigUnit;
					item_Items5.LastCost2 = (decimal?)value / num11;
					Item_Items item_Items6 = ItemDtails;
					value = Lastcost;
					convertMediumUnit = ItemDtails.ConvertBigUnit;
					item_Items6.LastCost = (decimal?)value / convertMediumUnit / ItemDtails.ConvertMediumUnit;
				}
				((DbContext)gTSdbEntities).SaveChanges();
			}
			finally
			{
				((IDisposable)gTSdbEntities)?.Dispose();
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	public int InsertOrders(out int Code, int SupplierID, string SupplierName, string SupplierPhone, string SupplierVatNum, DateTime Purchases_Date, int PurchType, int CostCenter, string Note, string NoteNum, decimal Tax, decimal TotalPrices, decimal Safy, decimal DiscountNum, decimal DiscountPerantage, decimal Tax_Discount, decimal TotalPrices_Discount, decimal Net, decimal Cash, decimal Bank, int Acc_Cash, int Acc_Bank, DataTable Items, decimal Charge, int ProjectId)
	{
		using (SqlCommand sqlCommand = new SqlCommand("Insert_Order_Purchases", con))
		{
			sqlCommand.CommandType = CommandType.StoredProcedure;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Clear();
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@BranchID", SqlDbType.Int).Value = LoginDetails.BranchID;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@SupplierID", SqlDbType.Int).Value = SupplierID;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@SupplierName", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = SupplierName;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@SupplierPhone", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = SupplierPhone;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@SupplierVatNum", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = SupplierVatNum;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Purchases_Date", SqlDbType.DateTime).Value = Purchases_Date;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Order_Paymant_Type", SqlDbType.Int).Value = PurchType;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@CostCentersID", SqlDbType.Int).Value = CostCenter;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Note", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = Note;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@NoteNum", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = NoteNum;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Tax", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = Tax;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@TotalPrices", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = TotalPrices;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Safy", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = Safy;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@DiscountNum", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = DiscountNum;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@DiscountPerantage", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = DiscountPerantage;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Tax_Discount", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = Tax_Discount;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@TotalPrices_Discount", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = TotalPrices_Discount;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Net", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = Net;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Cash", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = Cash;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Bank", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = Bank;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Acc_Cash", SqlDbType.Int).Value = Acc_Cash;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Acc_Bank", SqlDbType.Int).Value = Acc_Bank;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@UserID_Add", SqlDbType.Int).Value = LoginDetails.UserID;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@UserBranch_Add", SqlDbType.Int).Value = LoginDetails.BranchID;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@UserMacAddress_Add", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = LoginDetails.macAddress;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@SalesMan", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = LoginDetails.UserName;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Charge", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = Charge;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@ProjectId", SqlDbType.Int).Value = ProjectId;
			DataTable changes = Items.GetChanges(DataRowState.Added);
			sqlCommand.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Items", changes);
			SqlParameter sqlParameter = sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Return Value", SqlDbType.Int);
			sqlParameter.Direction = ParameterDirection.ReturnValue;
			sqlCommand.CommandTimeout = 999999;
			if (con.State == ConnectionState.Closed)
			{
				con.Open();
			}
			Code = sqlCommand.ExecuteNonQuery();
			object value = sqlParameter.Value;
			con.Close();
			Code = Convert.ToInt32(value);
		}
		return Code;
	}

	public int UpdateOrders(int Code, int SupplierID, string SupplierName, string SupplierPhone, string SupplierVatNum, DateTime Purchases_Date, int PurchType, int CostCenter, string Note, string NoteNum, decimal Tax, decimal TotalPrices, decimal Safy, decimal DiscountNum, decimal DiscountPerantage, decimal Tax_Discount, decimal TotalPrices_Discount, decimal Net, decimal Cash, decimal Bank, int Acc_Cash, int Acc_Bank, DataTable Items, decimal Charge, int projectId)

			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@SalesMan", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = LoginDetails.UserName;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Charge", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = Charge;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@projectId", SqlDbType.Decimal).Value = projectId;
			DataTable changes = Items.GetChanges(DataRowState.Added);
			sqlCommand.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Items", changes);
			SqlParameter sqlParameter = sqlCommand.Parameters.Add("@Return Value", SqlDbType.Int);
			sqlParameter.Direction = ParameterDirection.ReturnValue;
			sqlCommand.CommandTimeout = 999999;
			if (con.State == ConnectionState.Closed)
			{
				con.Open();
			}
			result = sqlCommand.ExecuteNonQuery();
			con.Close();
			result = 1;
		}

	}

	public int DeleteOrders(int PurBranchID)
	{
		int result = 0;
		using (SqlCommand sqlCommand = new SqlCommand("Delete_Order_Purchases_ALL", con))
		{
			sqlCommand.CommandType = CommandType.StoredProcedure;
			sqlCommand.Parameters.Clear();

			sqlCommand.CommandTimeout = 999999;
			if (con.State == ConnectionState.Closed)
			{
				con.Open();
			}
			result = sqlCommand.ExecuteNonQuery();
			con.Close();
			result = 1;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public bool IsItemVat(int ItemID)
	{
		GTSdbEntities db = new GTSdbEntities();
		try
		{
			return (from s in (IQueryable<TblSetting>)db.TblSettings
				from i in (IEnumerable<Item_Items>)db.Item_Items
				where i.ItemId == ItemID
				select (s.IsVat == (bool?)true && s.ItemTotalWithVat == (bool?)false && i.Is_Tax == (bool?)false) ? ((bool?)false) : ((bool?)null)).FirstOrDefault() ?? true;
		}
		finally
		{
			if (db != null)
			{
				((IDisposable)db).Dispose();
			}
		}
	}
}

		public decimal SmallUnitPrice { get; set; }

		public decimal UnitPrice { get; set; }

		public string UnitType { get; set; }
	}

	private int CachCode = 0;

	private DataTable dtCurrentTable = new DataTable();

	private GTSdbEntities db = new GTSdbEntities();
